### [1] [Generalised Cartan Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21809)
*David Osten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper presents a Cartan-geometric formulation of generalized geometries using a differential graded Lie algebra (dgLa), extending the tangent bundle to incorporate both a global duality group and a local gauge group. It systematically constructs generalized connections, torsion, and curvature, and connects these structures to the phase space of branes in M-theory.
\n\n
本文提出了一种基于微分分次李代数（dgLa）的广义几何的嘉当几何框架，通过扩展切丛以同时包含全局对偶群和局部规范群。该框架系统地构建了广义联络及其挠率与曲率张量，并将这些代数结构与M理论中膜的相空间联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard Cartan geometry describes classical spacetime geometries via a principal bundle with structure group $H$ and a Cartan connection taking values in a Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g} \supset \mathfrak{h}$. However, in string theory and M-theory, dualities and extended objects (branes) necessitate geometries beyond Riemannian or even standard generalized geometry (e.g., Double Field Theory, Exceptional Field Theory). The motivation is to develop a unified, mathematically rigorous framework—based on dgLa—that naturally incorporates both global U-duality symmetries and local gauge redundancies inherent in such extended geometries.
\n\n
标准嘉当几何通过具有结构群 $H$ 的主丛和取值于李代数 $\mathfrak{g} \supset \mathfrak{h}$ 的嘉当联络来描述经典时空几何。然而，在弦论和M理论中，对偶性和延展物体（膜）要求超越黎曼几何甚至标准广义几何（如双场论、例外场论）的新几何结构。本文旨在构建一个基于微分分次李代数（dgLa）的统一且数学上严谨的框架，自然地融合此类延展几何中固有的全局U-对偶对称性与局部规范冗余。

Method: The authors generalize Cartan geometry by replacing the usual Lie algebra data with a differential graded Lie algebra (dgLa). They extend the tangent bundle $TM$ to a higher bundle whose fibers carry representations of both a global duality group (e.g., $E_{d(d)}$ in M-theory) and a local gauge group. Using the dgLa structure, they define a generalized connection as a degree-1 element satisfying a Maurer–Cartan-type equation $d\\omega + \\frac{1}{2}[\\omega, \\omega] = 0$ up to homotopy. Torsion and curvature are then derived from the failure of this equation to hold strictly, encoded in the differential of the dgLa. The construction is applied to the phase space of M2- and M5-branes, where the dgLa encodes the algebra of brane charges and diffeomorphisms.
\n\n
作者通过将通常的李代数数据替换为微分分次李代数（dgLa）来推广嘉当几何。他们将切丛 $TM$ 扩展为一个高阶丛，其纤维同时携带全局对偶群（例如M理论中的 $E_{d(d)}$）和局部规范群的表示。利用dgLa结构，他们将广义联络定义为满足莫勒–嘉当型方程 $d\\omega + \\frac{1}{2}[\\omega, \\omega] = 0$（在同伦意义下）的度为1的元素。挠率与曲率则由该方程严格成立性的破坏所导出，由dgLa的微分算子编码。该构造被应用于M2-膜和M5-膜的相空间，其中dgLa编码了膜荷与微分同胚的代数结构。

Result: The paper successfully constructs a generalised Cartan connection for arbitrary dgLa-based geometries, along with well-defined torsion and curvature tensors. These objects transform covariantly under both the global duality group and the local gauge group. When specialized to M-theory, the framework reproduces known structures on the phase space of branes, such as the Courant algebroid for M2-branes and its exceptional generalisations for M5-branes, now understood as manifestations of the underlying dgLa-Cartan geometry.
\n\n
本文成功构建了适用于任意基于dgLa的几何的广义嘉当联络，以及良定义的挠率与曲率张量。这些对象在全局对偶群和局部规范群下协变变换。当应用于M理论时，该框架重现了膜相空间上的已知结构，例如M2-膜的Courant代数胚及其对M5-膜的例外推广，现在被理解为底层dgLa-嘉当几何的具体表现。

Conclusion: The proposed dgLa-based Cartan geometry provides a powerful and flexible formalism unifying global duality symmetries and local gauge structures in extended geometries arising in M-theory. It offers a systematic method to define connections and curvature beyond conventional geometry, paving the way for a deeper understanding of the geometric foundations of quantum gravity and brane dynamics.
\n\n
所提出的基于dgLa的嘉当几何为统一M理论中出现的延展几何里的全局对偶对称性与局部规范结构提供了一个强大而灵活的形式体系。它提供了一种系统方法来定义超越传统几何的联络与曲率，为深入理解量子引力与膜动力学的几何基础开辟了道路。

Abstract: This talk introduces a Cartan-geometric framework for generalised geometries governed by a differential graded Lie algebra. In contrast to ordinary Cartan geometry, the tangent bundle is extended and qu both a global duality group and a local gauge group. This framework provides a systematic construction of generalised connections and their torsion and curvature tensors for generic generalised geometries. We also review the realisation of these algebraic structures on the phase space of branes in M-theory.

</details>


### [2] [New mechanism for fermion localization in $f(T,T_G)$-brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21838)
*Allan R. P. Moreira,Fernando M. Belchior,Guo-Hua Sun,Shi-Hai Dong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies fermion localization in a 5D braneworld within $f(T,T_G)$ modified teleparallel gravity, showing that the teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet term $T_G$ alters effective potentials, enabling chiral zero-mode localization and generating resonant massive states; information-theoretic tools confirm enhanced localization due to torsional corrections.
\n\n
本文研究了在 $f(T,T_G)$ 改进的teleparallel引力框架下，五维brane世界中费米子的局域化问题，表明teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet项 $T_G$ 改变了有效势，使得手征零模得以局域化并产生共振的有质量态；信息论工具证实了torsion修正增强了局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore how higher-order torsional invariants—specifically the teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet term $T_G$—affect fermionic field localization in braneworld models, extending beyond standard General Relativity and minimal coupling scenarios. The authors aim to understand whether non-minimal couplings in $f(T,T_G)$ gravity can yield viable mechanisms for localizing chiral fermions on the brane and produce observable resonance structures.
\n\n
动机是探索高阶torsion不变量——特别是teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet项 $T_G$ ——如何影响brane世界模型中的费米子场局域化，超越标准广义相对论和最小耦合情形。作者旨在理解 $f(T,T_G)$ 引力中的非最小耦合是否能提供可行的手征费米子brane局域化机制并产生可观测的共振结构。

Method: The authors consider a 5D braneworld with a Dirac spinor non-minimally coupled to torsional invariants in an $f(T,T_G)$ gravity framework. They derive the 5D Dirac equation, decompose it into Kaluza-Klein (KK) modes, and reduce it to effective Schrödinger-like equations for each chirality. The resulting effective potentials incorporate contributions from both torsion scalar $T$ and Gauss-Bonnet analog $T_G$. They analyze zero-mode normalizability and search for resonant states in the continuous massive spectrum. Additionally, they apply Shannon entropy and relative probability as information-theoretic diagnostics of localization strength.
\n\n
作者考虑一个五维brane世界，其中Dirac旋量与 $f(T,T_G)$ 引力框架下的torsion不变量非最小耦合。他们推导出五维Dirac方程，将其分解为Kaluza-Klein（KK）模，并约化为每个手征分量的有效Schrödinger型方程。所得有效势包含torsion标量 $T$ 和Gauss-Bonnet类比项 $T_G$ 的贡献。他们分析零模的可归一化性，并在连续的有质量谱中寻找共振态。此外，他们采用Shannon熵和相对概率作为局域化强度的信息论诊断工具。

Result: The zero-mode analysis shows that only one chiral component can be localized on the brane, with confinement strength dependent on the specific $f(T,T_G)$ model. The massive KK spectrum is continuous, but potential wells support quasi-bound resonant states. Information-theoretic measures reveal that torsional modifications—especially from $T_G$—lead to a nontrivial redistribution of probability density, resulting in stronger localization compared to pure $f(T)$ or Einstein gravity cases.
\n\n
零模分析表明，仅有一个手征分量可在brane上局域化，其束缚强度依赖于具体的 $f(T,T_G)$ 模型。有质量KK谱是连续的，但势阱支持准束缚的共振态。信息论度量揭示，torsion修正——尤其是来自 $T_G$ 的贡献——导致概率密度的非平凡重分布，相比纯 $f(T)$ 或Einstein引力情形表现出更强的局域化。

Conclusion: Higher-order torsional terms, particularly the teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet invariant $T_G$, play a crucial role in shaping fermion localization and resonance phenomena in braneworlds. Non-minimal coupling in $f(T,T_G)$ gravity provides a viable mechanism for chiral fermion localization, and information-theoretic tools offer a robust quantitative framework to assess localization efficiency. This underscores the phenomenological richness of modified teleparallel gravity beyond Riemannian formulations.
\n\n
高阶torsion项，尤其是teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet不变量 $T_G$，在塑造brane世界中费米子局域化和共振现象方面起着关键作用。$f(T,T_G)$ 引力中的非最小耦合为手征费米子局域化提供了可行机制，而信息论工具为评估局域化效率提供了稳健的定量框架。这凸显了改进teleparallel引力相对于黎曼几何表述的唯象丰富性。

Abstract: We investigate the localization of fermionic fields in a five-dimensional braneworld scenario within the framework of modified teleparallel gravity described by a general $f(T,T_G)$ function. Considering a non-minimal coupling between a Dirac spinor and the torsional invariants, we derive the effective Schrödinger-like equations governing the Kaluza-Klein modes. We showed that the contribution of the teleparallel Gauss-Bonnet term significantly modifies the effective potentials and, consequently, the localization properties. The zero-mode analysis reveals that only one chiral component can be localized on the brane, with the degree of confinement depending on the chosen model. In the massive sector, the spectrum is continuous, but resonant states arise due to the internal structure of the potentials. Additionally, we employ information-theoretic measures, such as Shannon entropy and relative probability, to quantify the localization mechanism. Our results show that the torsional modifications induce a nontrivial redistribution of information, exhibiting stronger localization. These findings highlight the role of higher-order torsional terms in shaping fermionic localization and resonance structures in braneworld scenarios.

</details>


### [3] [Stochastic inflation as an open quantum system II: open effective field theory and stochastic matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21929)
*Yue-Zhou Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper advances the open quantum system interpretation of stochastic inflation by formulating an open effective field theory (open EFT) for the reduced density matrix of super-Hubble modes. It reveals two distinct renormalization group (RG) flows: the standard Wilsonian RG and a novel stochastic RG stemming from the system's openness. The authors identify Gaussian and non-Gaussian diffusion terms as effective operators in the influence functional, matched via a time-domain method-of-regions. Beyond Gaussian order, Wilson coefficients become nonlocal, non-Markovian kernels. They derive nonlocal functional master equations—including Fokker-Planck and Klein-Kramers equations—and initiate a continuum formulation using an analytic regulator, demonstrating stochastic renormalization with a massive scalar example.
\n\n
本文通过为超哈勃模式的约化密度矩阵构建开放有效场论（open EFT），进一步发展了随机暴胀作为开放量子系统的诠释。文章揭示了两种不同的重整化群（RG）流：标准的Wilsonian RG 和源于系统开放性的新型随机 RG。作者识别出高斯与非高斯扩散项作为影响泛函中的有效算符，并通过时间域的method-of-regions方法与完整微扰理论的关联函数和形状因子匹配。在高斯阶以上，Wilson系数变为非局域、非马尔可夫的核。他们推导了非局域泛函主方程——包括对角密度矩阵的Fokker-Planck方程和Wigner泛函的Klein-Kramers方程，并初步构建了连续版本的open EFT，用解析正规子替代硬截断，以有质量标量场为例展示了随机重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to place stochastic inflation on firmer theoretical footing by treating it rigorously as an open quantum system, where short-wavelength (UV) modes act as an environment that decoheres and drives the long-wavelength (IR) system. Previous formulations lacked a systematic effective field theory framework incorporating both quantum and stochastic aspects. This work aims to construct such an open EFT for the reduced density matrix, clarify its renormalization structure—especially the interplay between conventional Wilsonian RG and a new stochastic RG—and establish precise matching conditions between the EFT and the full UV theory.
\n\n
动机在于通过将随机暴胀严格视为开放量子系统（其中短波长（UV）模作为环境导致长波长（IR）系统退相干并驱动其演化），为其奠定更坚实的理论基础。以往的表述缺乏同时包含量子与随机效应的系统性有效场论框架。本文旨在为约化密度矩阵构建这样的开放EFT，阐明其重整化结构——特别是传统Wilsonian RG与新型随机RG之间的相互作用——并建立EFT与完整UV理论之间的精确匹配条件。

Method: The authors construct an open effective field theory using the influence functional formalism, integrating out UV modes to obtain a nonlocal action for IR modes. They employ a hard momentum cutoff to separate scales and implement a time-domain method-of-regions to match EFT correlators to those computed in the full perturbative theory. This matching determines the structure of effective operators (Gaussian/non-Gaussian diffusion) in the influence functional. They then derive the bare Hamiltonian density from the influence functional and obtain functional master equations (Fokker-Planck for $\\rho[\\phi,\\phi]$ and Klein-Kramers for the Wigner functional). Finally, they replace the hard cutoff with an analytic regulator (e.g., exponential suppression) to define a continuum stochastic RG flow and demonstrate renormalization using a massive scalar field.
\n\n
作者采用影响泛函形式，通过对UV模积分得到IR模的非局域作用量，构建开放有效场论。他们使用动量硬截断分离尺度，并采用时间域的method-of-regions方法，将EFT关联函数与完整微扰理论计算结果匹配，从而确定影响泛函中有效算符（高斯/非高斯扩散）的结构。随后，他们从影响泛函导出裸哈密顿量密度，并得到泛函主方程（对角密度矩阵$\\rho[\\phi,\\phi]$的Fokker-Planck方程和Wigner泛函的Klein-Kramers方程）。最后，他们用解析正规子（如指数压低）替代硬截断，定义连续的随机RG流，并以有质量标量场为例展示重整化过程。

Result: The key results are: (1) identification of two RG flows—Wilsonian and stochastic—in open EFT; (2) demonstration that Gaussian and non-Gaussian diffusion arise as required effective operators matched to full-theory correlators and form factors; (3) proof that beyond Gaussian order, Wilson coefficients generalize to nonlocal, non-Markovian Wilson kernels; (4) derivation of nonlocal functional master equations, including the Fokker-Planck equation $$\\partial_t \\rho[\\phi,\\phi] = \\int d^3x \\left[ -\\frac{\\delta}{\\delta \\phi(\\mathbf{x})} (A[\\phi] \\rho) + \\frac{1}{2} \\frac{\\delta^2}{\\delta \\phi(\\mathbf{x})^2} (D[\\phi] \\rho) + \\cdots \\right]$$ and the Klein-Kramers equation for the Wigner functional; (5) successful implementation of a continuum stochastic RG using an analytic regulator, with explicit renormalization demonstrated for a massive scalar.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 在开放EFT中识别出两种RG流——Wilsonian和随机RG；(2) 证明高斯与非高斯扩散项是匹配完整理论关联函数和形状因子所必需的有效算符；(3) 证明在高斯阶以上，Wilson系数推广为非局域、非马尔可夫的Wilson核；(4) 推导出非局域泛函主方程，包括Fokker-Planck方程 $$\\partial_t \\rho[\\phi,\\phi] = \\int d^3x \\left[ -\\frac{\\delta}{\\delta \\phi(\\mathbf{x})} (A[\\phi] \\rho) + \\frac{1}{2} \\frac{\\delta^2}{\\delta \\phi(\\mathbf{x})^2} (D[\\phi] \\rho) + \\cdots \\right]$$ 以及Wigner泛函的Klein-Kramers方程；(5) 成功利用解析正规子实现连续随机RG，并以有质量标量场为例明确展示了重整化。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a consistent open effective field theory framework for stochastic inflation, unifying quantum decoherence and classical stochasticity through the language of open quantum systems. It reveals that stochastic renormalization is governed by nonlocal, non-Markovian dynamics beyond leading order, necessitating a generalization of Wilsonian EFT concepts. The continuum formulation paves the way for systematic higher-loop computations and connections to other approaches like the Cosmological Bootstrap or EFTofLSS. This framework provides a rigorous foundation for computing late-time cosmological correlators and understanding the quantum-to-classical transition during inflation.
\n\n
本文建立了随机暴胀的一致性开放有效场论框架，通过开放量子系统的语言统一了量子退相干与经典随机性。研究表明，随机重整化在领头阶之外由非局域、非马尔可夫动力学主导，这要求对Wilsonian EFT概念进行推广。连续表述为系统性的高圈计算以及与其他方法（如Cosmological Bootstrap或EFTofLSS）的联系铺平了道路。该框架为计算晚期宇宙学关联函数及理解暴胀期间的量子-经典过渡提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We further develop the proposal in Phys.\ Rev.\ Lett.\ \textbf{136} 071501 that interprets stochastic inflation as an open quantum system, by constructing the open effective field theory for the reduced density matrix of long wavelength modes. We clarify that this open effective field theory enjoys two renormalization group flows: the conventional Wilsonian channel, and a stochastic channel arising from the openness that has no counterpart in ordinary Wilsonian effective field theory. Focusing on the stochastic channel in the hard cutoff scheme, we identify both Gaussian and non-Gaussian diffusion as effective operators in the influence functional, and show that they are required by matching onto correlators and form factors of the perturbative full theory through a method-of-region in time. Beyond Gaussian order, the matching data are no longer local Wilson coefficients but nonlocal and non-Markovian Wilson kernels. We then obtain the bare Hamiltonian density of this open effective field theory and derive its nonlocal functional master equations, including the Fokker-Planck equation for the diagonal density matrix and the Klein-Kramers equation for the Wigner functional, with their zero-modess simplifications discussed. Finally, we take a first step toward a continuum version of this open effective field theory, replacing the hard cutoff by an analytic regulator in the stochastic channel, and demonstrate stochastic renormalization using a massive scalar as an example.

</details>


### [4] [Krakow Lectures on Scalar Quantum Solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22285)
*Jarah Evslin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper provides a pedagogical overview of Linearized Soliton Perturbation Theory (LSPT), a Hamiltonian framework for computing quantum corrections to soliton states—specifically kinks and domain walls—by treating them as squeezed coherent states with perturbative loop corrections, and introduces a suitable inner product for scattering calculations involving non-normalizable momentum eigenstates.
\n\n
本文对线性化孤子微扰理论（Linearized Soliton Perturbation Theory, LSPT）进行了教学式综述，该理论是一种哈密顿方法，通过将孤子态（特别是扭结和畴壁）视为压缩相干态并加入微扰圈图修正来计算量子修正，并引入了一种适用于非正规化动量本征态的内积以用于散射计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional perturbative methods around the vacuum fail for solitonic sectors due to non-perturbative boundary conditions; LSPT addresses this by constructing soliton states directly in a quantum field theory framework, enabling systematic computation of multi-loop corrections to masses and scattering amplitudes.
\n\n
传统的真空附近微扰方法因孤子解具有非微扰边界条件而在孤子区域失效；LSPT 通过在量子场论框架中直接构造孤子态，从而能够系统地计算质量和散射振幅的多圈修正。

Method: The authors employ a Hamiltonian formulation where quantum soliton states are built as squeezed coherent states based on the classical soliton solution, followed by perturbative corrections organized in loops. They define a generalized inner product that accommodates non-normalizable momentum eigenstates, which is crucial for computing matrix elements like those in kink-meson scattering.
\n\n
作者采用哈密顿形式，将量子孤子态基于经典孤子解构造成压缩相干态，并在此基础上组织圈图级别的微扰修正。他们定义了一种广义内积，可处理非正规化的动量本征态，这对于计算扭结-介子散射等矩阵元至关重要。

Result: The paper demonstrates how LSPT yields explicit multi-loop corrections to soliton masses and enables consistent calculation of scattering processes such as kink-meson interactions using the newly introduced inner product. It also extends the formalism to higher-dimensional domain wall solitons.
\n\n
本文展示了 LSPT 如何给出孤子质量的显式多圈修正，并利用新引入的内积一致地计算扭结-介子相互作用等散射过程。该形式体系也被推广至高维的畴壁孤子。

Conclusion: Linearized Soliton Perturbation Theory provides a robust and systematic Hamiltonian approach to quantizing solitons beyond leading order, offering a practical framework for precision computations in non-perturbative sectors of quantum field theory.
\n\n
线性化孤子微扰理论为超越领头阶的孤子量子化提供了一个稳健且系统的哈密顿方法，为量子场论中非微扰区域的高精度计算提供了实用框架。

Abstract: We give a pedagogical introduction to Linearized Soliton Perturbation Theory (LSPT), a new and efficient tool for calculations involving quantum solitons. It is a Hamiltonian approach with a focus on explicitly constructing the soliton states. These states are squeezed, coherent states plus perturbative corrections. We will describe multi-loop corrections to states and their masses. An inner product suitable for non-normalizable momentum eigenstates will be introduced and applied to kink-meson scattering. We will also discuss domain wall solitons.

</details>


### [5] [Entanglement viscosity to entropy density ratio for spin-3/2 theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22409)
*R. V. Khakimov,G. Yu. Prokhorov,O. V. Teryaev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates whether the entanglement shear viscosity of the Minkowski vacuum, as perceived by an accelerated observer, universally saturates the KSS bound for higher-spin fields. Focusing on spin-$3/2$ Rarita-Schwinger-Adler (RSA) fields, the authors find a negative entanglement shear viscosity and negative entropy density via the modular Hamiltonian expansion, yet their ratio still saturates the KSS bound $\\eta/s = 1/4\\pi$. Using the Zubarev density operator formalism yields positive entropy, highlighting scheme dependence. The RSA theory is also shown to possess conformal field theory-like properties.
\n\n
本文研究了Minkowski真空在加速观测者看来的纠缠剪切粘度是否对高自旋场普遍满足Kovtun-Son-Starinets（KSS）界。作者聚焦于自旋为$3/2$的Rarita-Schwinger-Adler（RSA）场，通过模哈密顿量展开方法发现其纠缠剪切粘度和熵密度均为负值，但二者之比仍饱和KSS界$\\eta/s = 1/4\\pi$。采用Zubarev密度算符方法则得到正熵，表明结果具有方案依赖性。此外，论文还指出RSA理论具有共形场论（conformal field theory）的诸多特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent work showed that the Unruh thermal medium for lower-spin quantum fields exhibits a non-zero entanglement shear viscosity saturating the KSS bound $\\eta/s = 1/4\\pi$, suggesting a deep connection between quantum entanglement, hydrodynamics, and holography. The authors aim to test whether this universality extends to higher-spin fields, specifically spin-$3/2$, which are less understood and may exhibit pathologies or novel features due to gauge redundancies and constraints.
\n\n

Method: The authors compute the entanglement shear viscosity $\\eta$ for spin-$3/2$ fields using the Rarita-Schwinger-Adler (RSA) formulation in Rindler spacetime. 作者在Rindler时空中使用Rarita-Schwinger-Adler（RSA）理论计算自旋为$3/2$场的纠缠剪切粘度$\\eta$。他们采用模哈密顿量展开方法推导熵密度$s$，并独立运用Zubarev非平衡统计算符方法交叉验证熵的计算。此外，他们还分析了RSA理论中应力-能量张量的对称性和迹性质，以评估其共形特性。

对于RSA自旋$3/2$场，通过模哈密顿量展开计算得到的纠缠剪切粘度$\\eta$和熵密度$s$均为负值，但其比值满足$\\eta/s = 1/4\\pi$，饱和KSS界。相比之下，Zubarev密度算符方法给出正的熵密度，表明熵的符号依赖于操作定义。此外，RSA理论的应力-能量张量在壳上是无迹且对称的，证实该理论具备共形场论的关键属性。

Conclusion: The saturation of the KSS bound persists even for higher-spin fields like spin-$3/2$, despite apparent pathologies such as negative viscosity and entropy in certain formulations. 即使对于自旋$3/2$等高自旋场，尽管在某些表述中出现负粘度和负熵等表观病态，KSS界的饱和性依然成立，这表明纠缠-流体力学对应关系具有鲁棒性。然而，热力学量符号的不确定性揭示了在约束性高自旋系统中定义熵的微妙之处。RSA理论的共形特性进一步支持其适用于研究弯曲或加速参考系中的全息与热力学性质。

Abstract: It is known that the Minkowski vacuum appears as a thermal medium to an accelerated observer due to the renowned Unruh effect. More recently, it has been shown that at least for lower-spin fields this medium also exhibits a non-zero "entanglement" shear viscosity, which saturates the fundamental Kovtun-Son-Starinets (KSS) bound. We test the universality of this result for higher spins by computing the entanglement viscosity for spin-3/2 fields within the Rarita-Schwinger-Adler (RSA) theory. Strikingly, we obtain a negative viscosity. However, computing the entropy density using the modular Hamiltonian expansion method, we find it is also negative, and the viscosity to entropy ratio saturates the KSS bound. 本文成功构建了与三叶结（$3_1$）、八字结（$4_1$）和$5_1$结对应的$\mathfrak{su}_3$经典shaded A-polynomial。这些多项式源于推广至秩2的CG chord形式体系，并在双缩放半经典极限下获得。该框架统一了$\mathfrak{su}_2$情形下的KCH augmentation polynomial与其高秩推广，并通过量子群表示论为定义任意Lie（超）代数$\mathfrak{g}$的量子A-polynomial $\hat{A}(\hat{\ell}, \hat{m})$提供了路径，尽管本文未显式计算量子版本。

Conclusion: The CG chord formalism provides a robust algebraic framework to generalize classical A-polynomials beyond $\mathfrak{su}_2$, linking knot invariants, 2d CFTs, and quantum groups. Abstract: Classical A-polynomials $A(\ell,m)$ define constraints on coordinates $\ell$ and $m$ in $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ (a complexification of $SU(2)$) character varieties associated to knot complements $S^3\setminus K$. Quantum A-polynomials $\hat A(\hat \ell,\hat m)$ are difference operators annihilating Jones polynomials believed to represent wave functions of 3d Chern-Simons theory with gauge group $SU(2)$ on a toroidal pipe surrounding the knot $K$ strand -- a boundary of the knot complements $S^3\setminus K$. We suggest a construction of classical shaded A-polynomials $A_a(\ell_b,m_c)$ associated to Lie groups $SU(N)$. We exploit a formalism of Clebsh-Gordan (CG) chords, where indices $a$, $b$, $c$ run over $1,\ldots,N-1$. CG chords have a natural interpretation in terms of 2d CFTs of WZW type, or, alternatively, in terms of quantum group $U_q(\mathfrak{su}_N)$. Primarily we aim at classical A-polynomials by considering a double scaling limit when $q=e^{\hbar}$, $\hbar\to 0$ and the representations are huge, in particular, highest weight vector components $w_i\to \infty$ so that $\hbar w_i\sim m_i$ remain finite. Still we expect the presented techniques would be helpful in deriving quantum A-polynomials for arbitrary Lie (super)algebras $\mathfrak{g}$. Also we discuss explicit examples of A-polynomials for knots $3_1$, $4_1$ and $5_1$ for $\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{su}_3$.

### [7] [Ising surface defects can get dirty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22628)
*António Antunes,Apratim Kaviraj,Baishali Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper studies boundary critical behavior of the $4-\\varepsilon$ dimensional Wilson-Fisher fixed point under a quenched random magnetic field localized on a 2D surface (co-dimension 1 when $\\varepsilon \\to 1$). Using the replica method, it shows the ordinary boundary condition is stable, but a new non-trivial ``dirty'' boundary fixed point emerges at finite disorder strength or tuned local temperature. The work also explores the logarithmic structure in the defect operator spectrum arising from replica symmetry breaking.
\n\n
本文研究了在淬火随机磁场（局域于二维表面，在$\\varepsilon \\to 1$时为余维1）作用下，$4-\\varepsilon$维Wilson-Fisher不动点的边界临界行为。通过replica方法，作者发现ordinary边界条件在无序下是稳定的，但存在一个新的非平凡“dirty”边界不动点，可通过调节无序强度或局域温度实现。文章还探讨了由replica对称破缺导致的缺陷算符谱中的对数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real magnetic materials like uniaxial ferromagnets belong to the 3d Ising universality class and are inevitably influenced by boundaries and quenched disorder (e.g., impurities or random fields). Understanding how such disorder modifies boundary critical phenomena—particularly whether new boundary fixed points emerge and how logarithmic correlations arise—is essential for connecting field-theoretic predictions to real-world systems. The $4-\\varepsilon$ expansion provides a controlled setting to address these questions analytically.
\n\n
实际磁性材料（如单轴铁磁体）属于3d Ising普适类，并不可避免地受到边界和淬火无序（如杂质或随机场）的影响。理解此类无序如何改变边界临界现象——特别是是否会涌现新的边界不动点以及对数关联如何产生——对于将场论预言与现实系统联系起来至关重要。$4-\\varepsilon$展开为此类问题提供了可控的解析框架。

Method: The authors employ the replica trick to handle the quenched random magnetic field localized on a co-dimension 1 defect. They study the boundary renormalization group (RG) flow of the replicated $O(n)$ model near the Wilson-Fisher fixed point in $4-\\varepsilon$ dimensions. By analyzing the beta functions for bulk and boundary couplings—including disorder-induced couplings—they identify fixed points corresponding to different boundary conditions. The emergence of logarithmic operators is examined through the degeneracy of scaling dimensions in the replica limit $n \\to 0$, which signals logarithmic conformal field theory (LCFT) structure.
\n\n
作者采用replica技巧处理局域于余维1缺陷上的淬火随机磁场。他们在$4-\\varepsilon$维中研究复制后的$O(n)$模型在Wilson-Fisher不动点附近的边界重整化群（RG）流。通过分析包括无序诱导耦合在内的体与边界耦合的beta函数，识别出对应不同边界条件的不动点。通过对数共形场论（LCFT）结构所要求的replica极限$n \\to 0$下标度维数的简并性，考察了对数算符的出现机制。

Result: The ordinary boundary fixed point remains stable under weak random surface magnetic fields. However, a new non-trivial boundary fixed point—the ``dirty'' boundary condition—emerges when either the disorder strength or the local surface temperature is tuned. At this new fixed point, the boundary operator spectrum exhibits logarithmic scaling behavior, characteristic of logarithmic CFTs, arising from the coalescence of operator dimensions in the replica limit. Explicit one-loop calculations confirm the existence and stability of both fixed points.

ordinary边界不动点在弱随机表面磁场下保持稳定。然而，当调节无序强度或局域表面温度时，会涌现出一个新的非平凡边界不动点——即“dirty”边界条件。在此新不动点处，边界算符谱展现出对数标度行为，这是对数CFT的特征，源于replica极限下算符维数的合并。一阶微扰计算明确证实了这两个不动点的存在与稳定性。

Conclusion: Quenched disorder localized on a boundary can induce new universality classes of surface critical behavior beyond the standard ordinary, special, and extraordinary transitions. The replica formalism not only captures the stability of conventional boundary conditions but also reveals novel ``dirty'' fixed points with logarithmic correlations. This demonstrates that realistic boundary disorder can qualitatively alter critical phenomena and enrich the landscape of boundary conformal field theories.

局域于边界的淬火无序可诱导出超越标准ordinary、special和extraordinary相变的新表面临界普适类。replica形式不仅捕捉了传统边界条件的稳定性，还揭示了具有对数关联的新“dirty”不动点。这表明现实的边界无序可定性地改变临界现象，并丰富边界共形场论的理论图景。

Abstract: Real critical systems, such as uniaxial ferromagnets in the 3d Ising universality class, are constrained by boundaries and subject to random couplings. We consider the Wilson-Fisher fixed point in $4-ε$ dimensions subject to a random magnetic field localized on a two-dimensional surface, which becomes co-dimension 1 in the physical $ε\to1$ limit. Using the replica method for the disordered field, we find that the ordinary boundary condition is stable under disorder but also discover a non-trivial ``dirty" boundary condition which can be reached by tuning the disorder strength or the local temperature. We also investigate the logarithmic structure of the defect spectrum and how it emerges via the replica formalism.

### [8] [Bottom-up open EFT for non-Abelian gauge theory with dynamical color environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22822)
*Yoshihiko Abe,Kanji Nishii*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: We construct a local open effective field theory (EFT) for non-Abelian gauge theories using the Schwinger–Keldysh formalism by explicitly retaining slow environmental degrees of freedom—specifically a dynamical color-frame (Stückelberg-like) field and an associated color-current sector—instead of integrating them out into a nonlocal influence functional. This yields a gauge-covariant Markovian embedding of non-Markovian color dynamics, from which nonlocal dissipative kernels and stochastic noise emerge upon integrating out the environment with retarded boundary conditions. \n\n
我们基于Schwinger–Keldysh形式体系构建了一个局域的开放有效场论（EFT）用于非阿贝尔规范理论，其关键在于显式保留慢环境自由度——特别是动力学色标架（类Stückelberg）场及其关联的色流扇区——而非将其完全积分掉形成非局域影响泛函。该构造实现了对非马尔可夫色响应的规范协变马尔可夫嵌入，在以推迟边界条件积分掉环境变量后，系统约化理论自然获得非局域耗散核与随机源。硬热圈（HTL）有效作用量在此框架下作为特定极限自然出现。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional approaches to open quantum systems in non-Abelian plasmas integrate out the environment entirely, leading to nonlocal influence functionals that obscure the physical origin of dissipation, memory, and fluctuations. Moreover, such formulations often lack manifest gauge covariance and struggle to incorporate dynamical environmental feedback. The authors aim to develop a bottom-up, local, and gauge-covariant open EFT that explicitly retains key slow environmental variables to transparently encode fluctuation-dissipation relations and memory effects while enabling systematic EFT extensions.
\n\n
传统处理非阿贝尔等离子体中开放量子系统的方法通常将环境完全积分掉，导致非局域的影响泛函，这掩盖了耗散、记忆效应和涨落的物理起源。此外，这类表述往往缺乏显式的规范协变性，且难以纳入动力学环境反馈。作者旨在构建一个自下而上、局域且规范协变的开放EFT，通过显式保留关键的慢环境变量，以透明地编码涨落-耗散关系与记忆效应，并为系统性的EFT扩展提供基础。

Method: The authors employ the Schwinger–Keldysh closed-time-path formalism to construct a local system-environment EFT. They introduce explicit environmental degrees of freedom: a dynamical color-frame field $R^a_{\ b}(x)$ (analogous to a Stückelberg field) and a color-current sector $J^a(x)$, coupled minimally to the Yang–Mills system. This yields a local Lagrangian with gauge-covariant interactions between system and environment. By imposing retarded boundary conditions on environmental fields and integrating them out, they derive the reduced system action featuring nonlocal retarded self-energies (dissipative kernels) and stochastic noise terms consistent with the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. The hard thermal loop (HTL) effective action emerges when the environmental response matches thermal equilibrium correlations.
\n\n
作者采用Schwinger–Keldysh闭合时间路径形式体系构建局域的系统-环境EFT。他们引入显式的环境自由度：动力学色标架场 $R^a_{\ b}(x)$（类Stückelberg场）和色流扇区 $J^a(x)$，并与杨–米尔斯系统做最小耦合。由此得到一个包含系统与环境之间规范协变相互作用的局域拉氏量。通过对环境场施加推迟边界条件并将其积分掉，导出约化系统作用量，其中包含非局域推迟自能（耗散核）和满足涨落-耗散定理的随机噪声项。当环境响应匹配热平衡关联时，硬热圈（HTL）有效作用量自然出现。

Result: The paper successfully constructs a gauge-covariant, local open EFT for non-Abelian gauge theories that explicitly incorporates environmental degrees of freedom. Upon integrating out the environment with retarded boundary conditions, the reduced system exhibits nonlocal dissipative kernels and stochastic sources obeying the fluctuation-dissipation relation. Crucially, the well-known hard thermal loop (HTL) effective action is recovered as a particular realization of the retained environmental response, validating the framework. The approach provides a transparent mechanism for encoding memory effects and color transport in hot non-Abelian plasmas within a local EFT language.
\n\n
本文成功构建了一个规范协变、局域的非阿贝尔规范理论开放EFT，显式纳入了环境自由度。在以推迟边界条件积分掉环境后，约化系统展现出满足涨落-耗散关系的非局域耗散核与随机源。关键的是，著名的硬热圈（HTL）有效作用量被重现为所保留环境响应的一个特例，验证了该框架的有效性。该方法为在局域EFT语言中透明地描述高温非阿贝尔等离子体中的记忆效应与色输运提供了机制。

Conclusion: This work establishes a novel bottom-up framework for open effective field theories of non-Abelian gauge theories, where slow environmental variables are retained as explicit dynamical fields. The resulting local EFT maintains gauge covariance, naturally incorporates fluctuation-dissipation structure, and reproduces established results like HTL as limiting cases. It offers a systematic foundation for developing dissipative Yang–Mills EFTs with dynamical environments, applicable to nonequilibrium color dynamics in quark-gluon plasmas and early-universe cosmology.
\n\n
本工作建立了一种新颖的自下而上非阿贝尔规范理论开放有效场论框架，其中慢环境变量被保留为显式动力学场。所得局域EFT保持规范协变性，自然包含涨落-耗散结构，并在极限情形下重现HTL等已知结果。该框架为构建含动力学环境的耗散型杨–米尔斯EFT提供了系统性基础，可应用于夸克-胶子等离子体及早期宇宙中的非平衡色动力学研究。

Abstract: We develop a bottom-up open effective field theory (EFT) for non-Abelian gauge theories within the Schwinger--Keldysh formalism. Instead of integrating out the environment completely and starting from a nonlocal influence functional, we retain the slow environmental response variables explicitly and construct a local system-environment EFT. The environmental sector is described by a dynamical color-frame variable, Stückelberg-like field, and an associated color-current sector, which gives the nontrivial interactions and dissipation between the system and the environment. The resulting construction provides a gauge-covariant Markov embedding of nonlocal and non-Markovian color response. After integrating out the retained environmental variables with retarded boundary conditions, the reduced system theory acquires nonlocal dissipative kernels and stochastic sources. Motivation: The chiral Lagrangian effectively describes low-energy QCD dynamics via pion interactions, but its higher-derivative couplings (encoded in Wilson coefficients) are only weakly constrained by traditional methods like current algebra or unitarity. Recent advances in the scattering amplitudes program—particularly dispersion relations, analyticity, and positivity—offer sharper tools to constrain effective field theories (EFTs). Chiral Lagrangian通过π介子相互作用有效描述低能QCD动力学，但其高阶导数耦合（编码在Wilson系数中）仅受传统方法（如流代数或幺正性）的弱约束。散射振幅领域的新进展——特别是色散关系、解析性和正性条件——为约束有效场论（EFT）提供了更锐利的工具。作者旨在应用这些基于振幅的现代方法，揭示Chiral Anomaly与非反常算符之间隐藏的一致性条件。

Method: The authors employ general principles from the modern scattering amplitudes program: analyticity, unitarity, and crossing symmetry, combined with dispersion relations for $2\\to2$ pion scattering amplitudes. In the planar limit (large-$N_c$), they construct a double spectral representation and apply the Froissart bound to ensure convergence. By expanding the amplitude in Mandelstam variables $s$ and $t$, they relate the Taylor coefficients to Wilson coefficients of the chiral Lagrangian. Positivity of the spectral function then yields inequalities among these coefficients. Crucially, they isolate the contribution from the Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) term—which encodes the chiral anomaly—and show it provides a non-vanishing subtraction constant that sets a lower bound on certain even-dimension operators.
\n\n
作者采用现代散射振幅程序中的基本原理：解析性、幺正性和交叉对称性，并结合$2\\to2$ π介子散射振幅的色散关系。在平面极限（大$N_c$）下，他们构建双谱表示并应用Froissart界以确保收敛性。通过对Mandelstam变量$s$和$t$展开振幅，将泰勒系数与Chiral Lagrangian的Wilson系数关联起来。谱函数的正性由此导出这些系数之间的不等式。关键在于，他们分离出Wess-Zumino-Witten（WZW）项的贡献——该贡献编码了Chiral Anomaly——并证明其提供了一个非零的减法常数，从而为某些偶维算符设定了下界。

Result: The paper establishes that in the planar limit, certain Wilson coefficients $c_i$ of dimension-eight operators in the chiral Lagrangian satisfy bounds of the form $c_i \\geq \\kappa \\cdot a_{\\text{anom}}^2$, where $a_{\\text{anom}}$ is fixed by the chiral anomaly (via the WZW term) and $\\kappa > 0$ is a theory-independent constant. This demonstrates that non-anomalous couplings cannot be arbitrarily small—they are bounded below by anomaly-induced effects. \n\n
本文证明，在平面极限下，Chiral Lagrangian中某些八维算符的Wilson系数$c_i$满足形如$c_i \\geq \\kappa \\cdot a_{\\text{anom}}^2$的约束，其中$a_{\\text{anom}}$由Chiral Anomaly（通过WZW项）固定，而$\\kappa > 0$为与理论无关的常数。这表明非反常耦合不能任意小——它们受到Anomaly诱导效应的下界限制。此外，作者提出了一种适用于任意平面树图EFT的广义“Multipositivity”条件，超越了Chiral Perturbation Theory的具体语境。

Conclusion: This work bridges the modern amplitudes program with low-energy QCD phenomenology, revealing that the chiral anomaly exerts nontrivial constraints on otherwise unconstrained higher-dimensional operators. Abstract: The chiral Lagrangian is a cornerstone of modern particle physics, offering a systematic and quantitative description of low-energy pions. In the planar limit, these constraints imply that certain Wilson coefficients of the chiral Lagrangian are bounded from below by the chiral anomaly.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-derivative quantum field theories generically suffer from the Ostrogradsky instability due to the presence of ghost degrees of freedom with negative norm or energy, which renders the Hamiltonian unbounded from below. Method: The authors employ the Keldysh-Lindblad formalism for open quantum systems to model the interaction between the ghost sector of a higher-derivative theory and Markovian dissipative baths. 作者采用开放量子系统的Keldysh-Lindblad形式来建模高阶导数理论中ghost扇区与马尔可夫耗散浴的相互作用。他们推导出非平衡Green函数的自洽能隙方程，得到动态生成的有效质量$m_{\\text{eff}}$和耗散宽度$\\gamma$。分析重点在于求解这些方程在不同耦合强度下的行为，以识别稳态解中的分岔和临界结构。

Result: Above a critical coupling strength, the gap equations admit two distinct dissipative steady-state branches: (i) a massive branch where a large $m_{\\text{eff}}$ preserves quasiparticle-like ghost excitations but shifts them out of the low-energy spectrum, and (ii) an overdamped branch where strong dissipation ($\\gamma \\gg m_{\\text{eff}}$) destroys quasiparticle coherence, effectively removing ghosts from the physical spectrum. 当耦合强度超过临界值时，能隙方程允许两种不同的耗散稳态分支：(i) 一个大质量分支，其中大的$m_{\\text{eff}}$保留类准粒子的ghost激发，但将其移出低能谱；(ii) 一个过阻尼分支，其中强耗散（$\\gamma \\gg m_{\\text{eff}}$）破坏准粒子相干性，有效从物理谱中移除ghost。这种分岔标志着一个与ghost谱特征相关的非平衡耗散相变。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate higher-derivative quantum field theories and the problem of Ostrogradsky instability within an open-system Keldysh-Lindblad framework. Coupling the ghost sector to dissipative baths generates non-perturbative effective masses and dissipative widths through self-consistent gap equations. Above a critical coupling, the nonequilibrium dynamics develops bifurcated dissipative branches, signaling the emergence of a dissipative phase transition and a nontrivial critical structure in parameter space. We find that the resulting dissipative dynamics can suppress ghost excitations through two distinct mechanisms: in one branch, a large dynamically generated effective mass preserves a quasiparticle-like excitation, while in the second branch, strong dissipative broadening destroys the quasiparticle character through overdamped dynamics. Our results suggest that dissipative effects may provide a nonequilibrium mechanism for the spectral suppression of Ostrogradsky ghosts. The comparison with the healthy sector indicates that the stabilization mechanism is intrinsically tied to the ghost-like spectral structure.

Abstract: Defects are both physically rich objects and powerful tools in modern quantum field theory. They are extended operators, such as boundaries, impurities, and probe particles, embedded in many-body systems. In this dissertation, we study the universal aspects of defect dynamics from the perspective of symmetry principles. We bring together several themes, including defect renormalization group flows, effective string theory, and impurities in atomic quantum gases.

Abstract: We provide some guidance and examples to clear up common misconceptions about special relativity. \n\n
本文构建了Lorentz gauge theory（LGT）中类Schwarzschild黑洞解的最大解析延拓，表明其因果结构与Schwarzschild时空相同，但当 $A_0 \\neq 1$ 时几何上不等价，其视界位置和表面引力由参数 $A_0$ 重新标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand how modifications from Lorentz gauge theory affect the global causal and geometric structure of black hole spacetimes, particularly whether such theories admit extensions analogous to the Kruskal-Szekeres completion of Schwarzschild spacetime.

旨在理解Lorentz gauge theory的修正如何影响黑洞时空的整体因果与几何结构，特别是这类理论是否允许类似于Schwarzschild时空的Kruskal-Szekeres完备化延拓。

Method: The authors analyze radial null geodesics in Schwarzschild-Droste (SD) and ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein (IEF) coordinates, then construct a Kruskal-Szekeres (KS)-type coordinate system adapted to the lapse function $f(r)=A_0^{-2}-2\\m/r$. 作者分析了Schwarzschild-Droste（SD）和ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein（IEF）坐标中的径向零测地线，随后针对lapse函数 $f(r)=A_0^{-2}-2\\m/r$ 构建了适配的Kruskal-Szekeres（KS）型坐标系，并进行了标准与正则Carter-Penrose（CP）紧致化以研究共形结构。

Result: The maximal analytic extension reveals two asymptotically flat exterior regions, a black-hole interior, and a white-hole interior—identical causal topology to Schwarzschild. However, physical quantities like horizon radius $r_+ = 2\\m A_0^2$ and surface gravity $\\kappa = 1/(4\\m A_0^4)$ depend explicitly on $A_0$, making the geometry inequivalent to Schwarzschild for $A_0 \\neq 1$.
\n\n
最大解析延拓揭示了两个渐近平直的外部区域、一个黑洞内部和一个白洞内部——其因果拓扑与Schwarzschild完全相同。然而，物理量如视界半径 $r_+ = 2\\m A_0^2$ 和表面引力 $\\kappa = 1/(4\\m A_0^4)$ 显式依赖于 $A_0$，因此当 $A_0 \\neq 1$ 时，该几何与Schwarzschild不等价。

Conclusion: Lorentz gauge theory admits a Schwarzschild-like black hole solution whose global causal structure is preserved under the KS extension, but its geometric properties are deformed by the Lorentz-violating parameter $A_0$, demonstrating that causal topology alone does not fix the physical spacetime geometry in modified gravity.

Lorentz gauge theory允许一个类Schwarzschild黑洞解，其在KS延拓下的整体因果结构得以保持，但其几何性质被Lorentz破坏参数 $A_0$ 所变形，表明在修改引力理论中，仅凭因果拓扑不足以确定物理时空几何。

Abstract: We study the maximal analytic extension of the Schwarzschild-like black hole solution in Lorentz gauge theory. The lapse function is $f(r)=A_0^{-2}-2\m/r$, so the horizon is located at $r_+=2\m A_0^2$ and the non-affinity coefficient of the horizon generator is $κ=1/(4\m A_0^4)$. We first analyze the radial null curves in the Schwarzschild-Droste (SD) and ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein (IEF) charts, and then construct the Kruskal-Szekeres (KS) chart adapted to the LGT geometry. The KS extension contains two exterior regions, a black-hole region and a white-hole region. We also present the standard and regular Carter-Penrose (CP) compactifications. The conformal skeleton is Schwarzschild-like, but the physical scale of the horizon, the surface gravity and the constant-radius curves remain controlled by $A_0$. Hence the solution has the same causal topology as Schwarzschild, while it is geometrically inequivalent to it when $A_0\neq1$.

</details>

TL;DR: This paper introduces a pedagogical framework using the Black Hole Vision smartphone app to teach General Relativity by analyzing simulated black hole images in Schwarzschild spacetime, enabling students to perform quantitative measurements such as mass estimation, Jacobian-based coordinate transformation analysis, Lyapunov exponent computation, and symmetry tests via eccentricity constraints.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to modernize General Relativity education by leveraging accessible mobile technology to provide hands-on, quantitative experiences with relativistic phenomena—specifically black hole imaging—that are typically only accessible through advanced simulations or research-level tools. 其动机是通过利用易于获取的移动技术，为学生提供对相对论现象（特别是黑洞成像）的动手定量体验，从而推动广义相对论教育的现代化。这些体验通常只能通过高级模拟或研究级工具获得，而该方法弥合了本科生和研究生在抽象理论与可观测效应之间的鸿沟。

Method: The method employs the Black Hole Vision app to simulate visual observations of a Schwarzschild black hole on a smartphone. Students perform hierarchical analyses: (1) triangulating the black hole mass via independent observational probes; (2) mapping anisotropic coordinate distortions using the Jacobian determinant of the lensing transformation; (3) computing the Lyapunov exponent from the exponential divergence of nearly bound photon orbits; (4) measuring integrated coordinate length to test global numerical consistency; and (5) applying sub-pixel metrology to constrain spacetime symmetry through eccentricity limits. All analyses follow standard metrological protocols to distinguish physical signals from instrumental noise.

该方法利用Black Hole Vision应用程序在智能手机上模拟Schwarzschild黑洞的视觉观测。学生进行分层分析：(1) 通过独立的观测探针三角测量黑洞质量；(2) 利用透镜变换的Jacobian行列式映射各向异性坐标畸变；(3) 从近束缚光子轨道的指数发散中计算Lyapunov指数；(4) 通过测量积分坐标长度检验全局数值一致性；(5) 应用亚像素计量学通过偏心率上限约束时空对称性。所有分析均遵循标准计量协议，以区分物理信号与仪器噪声。

Result: Students successfully estimate the simulated Schwarzschild mass with consistent results across multiple probes, quantify coordinate anisotropy via the Jacobian map, measure a Lyapunov exponent consistent with theoretical predictions ($\\lambda \\sim c^3/(GM)$), verify numerical consistency through integrated length invariance, and impose tight sub-pixel constraints on eccentricity ($e < 10^{-3}$), confirming spherical symmetry within simulation resolution.
\n\n
学生成功地通过多个探针一致地估计了模拟的Schwarzschild质量，通过Jacobian图量化了坐标各向异性，测得的Lyapunov指数与理论预测一致（$\\lambda \\sim c^3/(GM)$），通过积分长度不变性验证了数值一致性，并对偏心率施加了严格的亚像素约束（$e < 10^{-3}$），在模拟分辨率内确认了球对称性。

Conclusion: The integration of mobile-based relativistic imaging into curricula provides an effective, scalable, and experimentally grounded approach to teaching General Relativity, allowing students to engage with core concepts—such as spacetime curvature, orbital instability, and symmetry—through direct quantitative analysis while developing critical metrological skills.
\n\n
将基于移动设备的相对论成像融入课程，为教授广义相对论提供了一种有效、可扩展且基于实验的方法，使学生能够通过直接的定量分析接触时空曲率、轨道不稳定性及对称性等核心概念，同时培养关键的计量学技能。

Abstract: This paper utilizes the {\it Black Hole Vision} smartphone application to catalyze a pedagogical shift in General Relativity education through the quantitative analysis of simulated black hole imaging. Presented here for the Schwarzschild spacetime, the investigation is designed with a hierarchical modularity suitable for undergraduate students, with an expanded version intended for graduate courses in General Relativity or Relativistic Astrophysics. By transforming the mobile device into an educational relativistic imaging tool, we triangulate the simulated Schwarzschild mass through independent probes and characterize anisotropic coordinate transformations via a Jacobian map. Global numerical consistency is investigated through integrated coordinate length, while the exponential instability of nearly bound orbits is quantified through a measurement of the simulated Lyapunov exponent. Finally, symmetry is constrained through a sub-pixel constraint on eccentricity in the simulated spacetime. By integrating this statistical framework, the paper enables students to explore the distinction between physical signatures and instrumental noise using established metrological protocols.

u} F^{\\mu\
u}$ 和 $\\mathcal{G} = \\frac{1}{4} F_{\\mu\
u} {}^*F^{\\mu\
u}$ 来定位满足 $|\\vec{E}| > E_{\\text{c}}$ 的dyadoregion。随后，他们计算该区域内的总电磁能量，基于lobe取向引入一个几何beaming factor，并评估 $e^{+}e^{-}\\gamma$ 等离子体的热力学条件，以确定高效对产生所需的最小磁场强度。

This framework offers a more realistic description of pair-loaded jet launching in astrophysical settings.

在倾斜磁场中，旋转黑洞附近的真空击穿为伽马射线暴中心引擎相关的电子-正电子对产生提供了一种可行且增强的机制。磁场倾角在决定该过程效率和能量学方面起着决定性作用，非对齐构型通常有利于更高的对产额和更低的磁场要求。该框架为天体物理环境中富含正负电子对的喷流启动提供了更现实的描述。

Abstract: Electron-positron ($e^{+}e^{-}$) pair creation by vacuum breakdown around compact objects is believed to power high-energy astrophysical transients like gamma-ray bursts (GRBs). The dyadoregion, the region where the induced electric field exceeds the critical value $E_{\text{c}}=m_{e}^{2}c^{3}/(e\hbar)$, is identified via the electromagnetic invariants. We also estimate the electromagnetic energy available for pair creation and derive a beaming factor that allows a conversion between the intrinsic dyadoregion energy and the observed isotropic energy. The thermodynamic properties of the resulting electron-positron-photon ($e^{+}e^{-}γ$) plasma are included, revealing an initial magnetic dominance. The evaluation of the minimum magnetic field required shows that misaligned magnetic fields generally favor pair creation more than aligned ones.

### [17] [On the Presence of a Tertiary Compact Object in GW190814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21955)
*Lalit Pathak,Hemantakumar Phurailatpam,Achamveedu Gopakumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether line-of-sight acceleration (LOSA) or residual orbital eccentricity could explain anomalies in the gravitational wave event GW190814. Using 32 seconds of data and Bayesian inference with the IMRPhenomXPHM waveform model augmented by leading-order LOSA and eccentricity corrections, the authors find no strong evidence for either effect individually or jointly. A strong degeneracy between LOSA and eccentricity parameters explains non-zero posterior estimates in joint analyses, reconciling conflicting prior claims.
\n\n
本文研究视线方向加速度（line-of-sight acceleration, LOSA）或残余轨道偏心率是否能解释引力波事件 GW190814 中的异常现象。作者使用 32 秒的数据，基于增强 LO SA 和偏心率一阶修正项的 IMRPhenomXPHM 波形模型进行贝叶斯推断，发现无论是单独还是联合分析，均未发现这两种效应的显著证据。LOSA 与偏心率参数之间存在强简并性，这解释了联合分析中非零后验估计的来源，并调和了此前相互矛盾的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the observation that both line-of-sight acceleration (LOSA) due to a massive nearby companion and residual orbital eccentricity induce similar frequency-dependent phase shifts in the inspiral gravitational waveform at leading order. This degeneracy raises the possibility that one effect could masquerade as the other in transient gravitational wave signals like GW190814. Conflicting claims in the literature—some reporting evidence for LOSA using short data segments—necessitate a more robust analysis with longer data duration to clarify whether such effects are truly present.
\n\n
动机源于一个关键观察：由附近大质量伴星引起的视线方向加速度（LOSA）和残余轨道偏心率在并合前旋近阶段的引力波相位中，于主导阶均产生相似的频率依赖性修正。这种简并性意味着在 GW190814 等瞬态引力波信号中，一种效应可能被误认为是另一种。文献中存在相互矛盾的主张——部分研究使用较短数据段声称探测到 LOSA——因此有必要利用更长的数据时段进行更稳健的分析，以澄清这些效应是否真实存在。

Method: The authors perform Bayesian parameter estimation using the IMRPhenomXPHM waveform family as the baseline model for LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA data. They augment this model with analytic leading-order corrections for both LOSA (parameterized by $a/c$, where $a$ is the line-of-sight acceleration and $c$ is the speed of light) and residual eccentricity (parameterized by $e_0$, the eccentricity at a reference frequency). The analysis uses 32 seconds of strain data around GW190814, significantly longer than the 4-second segment used in prior work. They compute Bayes factors to compare models with and without these corrections and examine posterior correlations between $a/c$ and $e_0$.
\n\n
作者采用 IMRPhenomXPHM 波形族作为 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 数据的基线模型，进行贝叶斯参数估计。他们在该模型中加入 LOSA（以 $a/c$ 参数化，其中 $a$ 为视线方向加速度，$c$ 为光速）和残余偏心率（以参考频率处的偏心率 $e_0$ 参数化）的一阶解析修正项。分析使用了 GW190814 周围 32 秒的应变数据，远长于此前研究使用的 4 秒片段。他们计算了包含与不包含这些修正项模型之间的贝叶斯因子（Bayes factor），并考察了 $a/c$ 与 $e_0$ 后验分布之间的相关性。

Result: In a LOSA-only analysis, the Bayes factor relative to the baseline model is $\sim 0.22$, indicating no evidence for LOSA and contradicting Yang et al.'s claim based on 4-second data. In a joint analysis including both LOSA and eccentricity, informative posteriors are obtained with median values $a/c \approx -2.8 \times 10^{-3}~\mathrm{s}^{-1}$ and $e_0 \approx 0.11$. However, the Bayes factor for this joint model is only $\sim 0.64$, still disfavoring the extended model. Crucially, a strong anti-correlation is found between $a/c$ and $e_0$, confirming a theoretical degeneracy in their contribution to the inspiral Fourier phase.
\n\n
在仅包含 LOSA 的分析中，相对于基线模型的贝叶斯因子约为 0.22，表明无 LOSA 证据，与 Yang 等人基于 4 秒数据的主张相矛盾。在同时包含 LOSA 与偏心率的联合分析中，获得了具有信息量的后验分布，典型值为 $a/c \approx -2.8 \times 10^{-3}~\mathrm{s}^{-1}$ 和 $e_0 \approx 0.11$。然而，该联合模型的贝叶斯因子仅为约 0.64，仍不支持扩展模型。关键的是，研究发现 $a/c$ 与 $e_0$ 之间存在强负相关性，证实了二者在旋近阶段傅里叶相位贡献中的理论简并性。

Conclusion: The 32-second analysis of GW190814 provides no statistically significant evidence for either line-of-sight acceleration or residual eccentricity. The apparent non-zero estimates in joint analyses arise from a strong degeneracy between these two effects, which imprint nearly identical signatures on the gravitational waveform phase at leading order. This degeneracy likely explains previous conflicting claims and underscores the necessity of using sufficiently long data segments and physically motivated waveform extensions when testing exotic formation scenarios or environmental effects in compact binary mergers.
\n\n
对 GW190814 的 32 秒数据分析并未提供视线方向加速度或残余偏心率存在的统计显著证据。联合分析中出现的非零参数估计源于这两种效应之间的强简并性——它们在主导阶对引力波相位留下几乎相同的印记。这一简并性很可能解释了此前相互矛盾的主张，并强调在检验致密双星并合的奇异形成机制或环境效应时，必须使用足够长的数据片段和具有物理动机的波形扩展模型。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from merging compact binaries are sensitive to line-of-sight acceleration (LOSA) induced by a massive companion in their vicinity. Interestingly, the leading-order contributions of LOSA and residual orbital eccentricity to the Fourier phase of the inspiral waveform exhibit similar frequency dependence, raising the possibility that a small eccentricity could mimic LOSA effects in transient GW events such as GW190814. We perform Bayesian inference using the IMRPhenomXPHM waveform family as the baseline LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA waveform model, augmented with leading-order LOSA and residual eccentricity corrections while using 32 seconds of data associated with GW190814. For a LOSA-only analysis, we find no evidence for a non-zero LOSA effect in GW190814, with a Bayes factor relative to the baseline model of approximately 0.22, consistent with the findings of Hendriks et al. and in tension with the claim by Yang et al., who employed only 4 seconds of GW190814 data. In a joint analysis that includes both leading-order LOSA and eccentricity effects, we obtain informative posteriors for both parameters, with representative values a/c approximately -2.8 x 10^{-3} s^{-1} and e_0 approximately 0.11. However, the corresponding Bayes factor relative to the baseline model is approximately 0.64, suggesting that the 32-second data do not provide significant evidence for either LOSA or residual eccentricity in GW190814. Further, our Bayesian runs reveal a strong correlation between the LOSA and eccentricity parameters, indicating a significant degeneracy in their imprint on the inspiral phase. This finding is consistent with our theoretical arguments and most likely explains the non-zero parameter estimates obtained in the joint analysis.

</details>

*Dina Demissenova,Nurzada Beissen,Kuantay Boshkayev,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper extends the notion of physical acceptability for static spherically symmetric perfect-fluid solutions in general relativity by incorporating thermodynamic consistency. Using relativistic equilibrium thermodynamics, entropy functionals, and the Tolman temperature relation, the authors formulate thermodynamic acceptability conditions and demonstrate their applicability through the Tolman IV solution, which yields a finite, positive entropy consistent with thermal equilibrium.
\n\n
本文通过引入热力学一致性，拓展了广义相对论中静态球对称理想流体解的物理可接受性概念。作者利用相对论性平衡热力学、熵泛函以及Tolman温度关系，提出了一组热力学可接受性条件，并以Tolman IV解为例，证明其具有有限且为正的平衡熵泛函，与热平衡条件一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While many exact solutions to Einstein's equations exist for static spherically symmetric perfect fluids, only few satisfy standard physical acceptability criteria (e.g., regularity, positivity of energy density and pressure, subluminal sound speed). The authors argue that a complete assessment of physical viability should also include thermodynamic consistency—particularly whether the solution admits a well-defined equilibrium entropy compatible with the Tolman-Ehrenfest effect in gravitational fields.
\n\n
尽管存在大量静态球对称理想流体的爱因斯坦场方程精确解，但仅有少数满足标准物理可接受性判据（如正则性、能量密度与压强为正、声速低于光速）。作者认为，对物理解的完整评估还应包含热力学一致性——特别是该解是否允许一个与引力场中Tolman-Ehrenfest效应相容的明确定义的平衡熵。

Method: The authors employ relativistic equilibrium thermodynamics to define an entropy functional $S = \int s(r) \sqrt{-g}\, d^3x$, where $s(r)$ is the proper entropy density. They impose the Tolman temperature relation $T(r)\sqrt{g_{00}(r)} = \text{const}$ as a condition for thermal equilibrium in static spacetimes. Thermodynamic acceptability requires: (i) finiteness and positivity of total entropy $S$, (ii) compatibility of $s(r)$ with the equation of state and field equations, and (iii) consistency with the Tolman condition. They apply this framework to the Tolman IV metric and verify these criteria explicitly.
\n\n
作者采用相对论性平衡热力学，定义熵泛函 $S = \int s(r) \sqrt{-g}\, d^3x$，其中 $s(r)$ 为固有熵密度。他们将Tolman温度关系 $T(r)\sqrt{g_{00}(r)} = \text{const}$ 作为静态时空热平衡的条件。热力学可接受性要求：(i) 总熵 $S$ 有限且为正；(ii) $s(r)$ 与状态方程及场方程相容；(iii) 与Tolman条件一致。作者将此框架应用于Tolman IV度规，并显式验证了这些条件。

Result: The Tolman IV solution is shown to admit a well-defined, finite, and positive equilibrium entropy functional that satisfies the Tolman temperature condition. This demonstrates that the solution is not only mathematically valid and physically acceptable under classical criteria (Delgaty-Lake), but also thermodynamically consistent in the context of relativistic equilibrium thermodynamics.
\n\n
研究表明，Tolman IV解具有一个良好定义、有限且为正的平衡熵泛函，并满足Tolman温度条件。这表明该解不仅在数学上成立、在经典判据（Delgaty-Lake）下物理可接受，而且在相对论性平衡热力学框架下也具有热力学一致性。

Conclusion: Thermodynamic consistency—particularly the existence of a positive, finite equilibrium entropy compatible with the Tolman relation—provides a natural and essential extension to existing physical acceptability programs for relativistic stellar models. This criterion may help refine the classification of interior solutions and ensure their compatibility with fundamental thermodynamic principles in curved spacetime.
\n\n
热力学一致性——特别是存在一个与Tolman关系相容的正定有限平衡熵——为现有相对论恒星模型的物理可接受性方案提供了自然且必要的拓展。该判据有助于优化内解的分类，并确保其与弯曲时空中的基本热力学原理相容。

Abstract: Static spherically symmetric perfect-fluid solutions of Einstein's equations play a central role in relativistic astrophysics and stellar structure theory. While many exact solutions satisfy Einstein's equations mathematically, only a limited subset satisfies physically acceptable conditions such as regularity, positivity of matter variables, and causal sound propagation. In this work, the classical concept of physical acceptability is extended to include thermodynamic considerations. Using relativistic equilibrium thermodynamics, entropy functionals, and the Tolman temperature relation, we formulate a set of thermodynamic acceptability conditions for relativistic stellar models. The Tolman IV solution is analyzed as an explicit example. We show that this solution admits a finite and positive equilibrium entropy functional consistent with the Tolman equilibrium condition. This analysis suggests that thermodynamic consistency provides a natural extension of the Delgaty-Lake acceptability program and may constitute an essential criterion in the classification of relativistic interior solutions.

</details>

本文研究了在修改引力理论中引力波透镜效应的波光学（wave-optics）机制，其中引力波振幅满足一个与曲率耦合的传播方程。该机制改变了红外动力学，使得标准几何光学假设失效，并通过低频相移和散射振幅诠释提供了探测广义相对论（GR）偏离的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to explore how modifications to General Relativity (GR) affect gravitational-wave propagation beyond the geometric-optics limit. In modified gravity with curvature-dependent interactions, this assumption breaks down, necessitating a new wave-optics framework to capture infrared deviations invisible to geometric optics.

动机源于探索修改后的广义相对论（GR）如何影响引力波在几何光学极限之外的传播。标准透镜分析假设放大因子在频率趋于零时趋近于1——这一结论依赖于GR特有的传播结构。在具有曲率依赖相互作用的修改引力理论中，该假设不再成立，因此需要一个新的波光学框架来捕捉几何光学无法探测的红外偏离。

Method: The authors introduce a phenomenological curvature-coupled propagation equation for gravitational-wave amplitude, which reduces to GR in the geometric-optics regime but exhibits distinct infrared behavior. They analyze the low-frequency limit using an interacting static Green function and construct a finite-frequency completion via partial-wave expansion. They reinterpret the solution in a distorted-wave picture, where curvature effects are absorbed into a dressed propagation basis, leaving residual lensing encoded in frequency-dependent phase shifts. Finally, they connect the formalism to scattering amplitudes.
\n\n
作者引入了一个现象学的、与曲率耦合的引力波振幅传播方程，该方程在几何光学极限下还原为GR，但在红外区域表现出不同的行为。他们利用一个相互作用的静态Green函数分析低频极限，并通过分波（partial-wave）展开构建有限频率的完备描述。他们进一步将解重新诠释为畸变波（distorted-wave）图像，其中曲率效应被吸收到一个“穿衣”（dressed）传播基中，剩余的透镜效应则编码在依赖频率的相移中。最后，他们将该形式体系与散射振幅语言联系起来。

Result: The paper demonstrates that in curvature-coupled modified gravity, the amplification factor does not necessarily approach unity as $\\omega \\to 0$, contrary to GR expectations. The correct infrared description is governed by an interacting Green function, and the full wave-optics solution admits a distorted-wave interpretation with finite-frequency phase shifts. These effects are interpretable as scattering amplitudes, revealing propagation-level deviations from GR that are undetectable in geometric optics.
\n\n
论文证明，在曲率耦合的修改引力理论中，放大因子在$\\omega \\to 0$时不一定趋近于1，这与GR的预期相反。正确的红外描述由一个相互作用的Green函数主导，完整的波光学解可被诠释为带有有限频率相移的畸变波。这些效应可理解为散射振幅，揭示了几何光学完全无法探测的GR传播层面的偏离。

Conclusion: Wave-optics gravitational lensing provides a novel observational window into modified gravity by probing curvature-induced infrared modifications to wave propagation that are absent in geometric optics. The framework connects lensing phenomena to scattering theory and highlights the necessity of going beyond the eikonal approximation to test fundamental gravity theories with low-frequency gravitational waves.
\n\n
波光学引力透镜为探测修改引力理论提供了一个新颖的观测窗口，因为它能探测几何光学中不存在的、由曲率诱导的波传播红外修正。该框架将透镜现象与散射理论联系起来，并强调了在利用低频引力波检验基础引力理论时，必须超越程函（eikonal）近似。

Abstract: We initiate the study of gravitational-wave lensing in the wave-optics regime within modified gravity. We consider a phenomenological setup in which the gravitational-wave amplitude obeys a curvature-coupled propagation equation. This framework reproduces the standard GR behaviour in the geometric-optics regime, while leading to qualitatively different infrared dynamics. In particular, the usual argument implying that the amplification factor approaches unity in the zero-frequency limit no longer applies. This is due to the persistence of curvature-induced interactions in the infrared, which modify the natural propagation basis itself. As a result, the standard Fresnel treatment ceases to be valid at sufficiently low frequency. The correct infrared regime is instead controlled by an interacting static Green function, with a finite-frequency completion provided by a partial-wave formulation. We show that this structure admits an equivalent distorted-wave interpretation, in which the curvature interaction is absorbed into a dressed reference propagation basis, while the residual lensing effect is encoded in finite-frequency phase shifts. We further demonstrate that these phenomena admit a natural interpretation in the language of scattering amplitudes. Wave-optics lensing can therefore probe propagation-level departures from GR that remain entirely invisible in geometric optics.

Method: The authors employ functional methods from quantum field theory adapted to classical dynamics. This establishes an exact duality between two equivalent formulations of the same physical content, thereby providing a rigorous derivation rooted in well-established RG principles.

Result: The key result is the demonstration that the classical RG flow equation introduced in arXiv:2510.27676 is exactly dual—via a Legendre transform—to the classical version of the Polchinski equation. This proves that the flow equation is not merely an ansatz but follows rigorously from established RG formalism, confirming its validity as an exact, non-perturbative tool for computing the gravitational effective action in the two-body problem.

核心结果是证明了arXiv:2510.27676中引入的经典RG流方程通过Legendre变换与Polchinski方程的经典版本精确对偶。这表明该流方程并非仅为假设，而是严格源自已建立的RG形式体系，确认了其作为两体问题中计算引力有效作用量的精确、非微扰工具的有效性。

Conclusion: By embedding the classical RG equation within the rigorous framework of exact renormalisation group theory, this work confirms its foundational soundness and extends its credibility beyond perturbative checks. The duality with the Polchinski-type equation cements the classical RG approach as a principled, systematic, and powerful method for addressing non-linear dynamics in general relativity, particularly relevant for high-precision gravitational wave modeling.
\n\n
通过将经典RG方程嵌入精确重整化群理论的严格框架中，本文确认了其基础的合理性，并将其可信度扩展到微扰检验之外。与Polchinski型方程的对偶性巩固了经典RG方法作为处理广义相对论中非线性动力学的一种有原则、系统且强大的方法，尤其适用于高精度引力波建模。

Abstract: In a companion paper arXiv:2510.27676, we introduced a non-perturbative classical renormalisation group (RG) flow equation as a novel method for treating strongly interacting problems in general relativity, with a prominent application to the two-body problem. While we demonstrated that it reproduces perturbation theory, via the Post-Minkowskian (PM) expansion, and its computational efficiency in reproducing the 1PN Post-Newtonian action, its derivation was heuristic. In this work, we place this flow equation on a firm formal foundation. In particular, we demonstrate that a Legendre transform maps the classical analogue of the Polchinski equation precisely to our classical RG equation. This establishes a duality between equivalent, exact RG equations for the gravitational effective action. The result, combined with the successful applications in arXiv:2510.27676, solidifies the classical RG framework as a powerful and rigorous new approach to the general relativistic two-body problem and gravitational wave physics.
