### [21] [Holographic Dark Energy with Hubble Radius as an Infrared Cutoff in Einstein-Cartan Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22143)
*Yongjun Yun,Jungjai Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies non-interacting holographic dark energy (HDE) with the Hubble radius as the infrared cutoff within Einstein-Cartan gravity. By deriving Friedmann-like equations using a torsion scalar sourced by a Weyssenhoff spin fluid, the authors obtain a natural scaling $Φ \sim a^{-3}$ without ad hoc assumptions. The torsion shifts the HDE equation of state to negative values, enabling cosmic acceleration and allowing phantom crossing ($ω_X \simeq -1$) even in the weak torsion regime $|Φ/H| < 1$. The model predicts a weakening acceleration consistent with DESI data and modifies the cosmic distance duality relation via a torsion-induced deviation parameter $η \sim \int_{t_S}^{t_O} dt\, a^{-3}$, while preserving the standard redshift–scale factor relation.
\n\n
本文研究了在Einstein-Cartan引力中以Hubble半径为红外截断的非相互作用holographic dark energy（HDE）。通过引入由Weyssenhoff自旋流体产生的torsion scalar，作者导出了Friedmann型方程，并自然得到标度律 $Φ \sim a^{-3}$，无需人为假设。该torsion scalar将HDE的状态方程移向负值，从而实现宇宙加速膨胀，并在弱torsion区域 $|Φ/H| < 1$ 内允许状态方程穿越phantom分界线（$ω_X \simeq -1$）。该模型预言宇宙加速逐渐减弱，与近期DESI观测一致；同时修改了宇宙距离对偶关系，引入torsion诱导的偏离参数 $η \sim \int_{t_S}^{t_O} dt\, a^{-3}$，但保持标准红移–尺度因子关系不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from two key issues: (1) the need to resolve the ad hoc ansatz problem in previous torsion-based cosmological models, where the time evolution of the torsion scalar was assumed rather than derived; and (2) the challenge of achieving late-time cosmic acceleration in standard holographic dark energy models with the Hubble radius as the infrared cutoff, which typically yield a dust-like equation of state ($ω = 0$) insufficient for acceleration. Einstein-Cartan gravity provides a natural framework to incorporate spacetime torsion sourced by intrinsic spin, offering a theoretically grounded alternative to phenomenological assumptions.
\n\n
动机源于两个关键问题：(1) 此前基于torsion的宇宙学模型中，torsion scalar的时间演化通常依赖人为假设（ad hoc ansatz），缺乏理论基础；(2) 在以Hubble半径为红外截断的标准holographic dark energy模型中，通常得到尘埃状状态方程（$ω = 0$），不足以驱动晚期宇宙加速膨胀。Einstein-Cartan引力通过内禀自旋自然引入时空torsion，为解决上述问题提供了理论自洽的框架。

Method: The authors start from the Einstein-Cartan action coupled to a Weyssenhoff spin fluid, vary the action to obtain the field equations, and introduce a torsion scalar $Φ$ related to the spin density. They derive modified Friedmann-like equations in a spatially flat FLRW background with torsion. Using conservation laws for the spin fluid, they analytically determine the scaling $Φ \sim a^{-3}$ without imposing an ansatz. They then solve for the holographic dark energy density $ρ_X = 3c^2 M_{\\text{Pl}}^2 H^2$ (with $c=1$ for Hubble cutoff) in the presence of torsion, compute its effective equation of state $ω_X$, and analyze the cosmic expansion history. They also examine the impact of torsion on the cosmic distance duality relation $d_L = d_A (1+z)^2 (1+η)$, deriving the torsion-induced deviation parameter $η$ via integration along the photon geodesic.
\n\n
作者从耦合Weyssenhoff自旋流体的Einstein-Cartan作用量出发，通过变分得到场方程，并引入与自旋密度相关的torsion scalar $Φ$。他们在具有torsion的平坦FLRW背景下导出修正的Friedmann型方程。利用自旋流体的守恒律，解析地确定了 $Φ \sim a^{-3}$ 的标度行为，无需人为设定。随后，他们在torsion存在下求解holographic dark energy密度 $ρ_X = 3c^2 M_{\\text{Pl}}^2 H^2$（对Hubble截断取 $c=1$），计算其有效状态方程 $ω_X$ 并分析宇宙膨胀历史。此外，他们还研究了torsion对宇宙距离对偶关系 $d_L = d_A (1+z)^2 (1+η)$ 的影响，并通过对光子测地线积分导出torsion诱导的偏离参数 $η$。

Result: The key results are: (1) The torsion scalar naturally scales as $Φ \sim a^{-3}$ due to spin conservation, eliminating the need for an ad hoc ansatz. (2) In the non-interacting HDE scenario with Hubble cutoff, torsion shifts the effective equation of state from $ω_X = 0$ to negative values, enabling cosmic acceleration. (3) The equation of state can approach $ω_X \simeq -1$ and cross the phantom divide ($ω_X < -1$) even when torsion is weak ($|Φ/H| < 1$). (4) The model predicts a dynamical dark energy with gradually weakening acceleration, potentially consistent with DESI observations. (5) Torsion modifies the cosmic distance duality relation with a deviation parameter $η \sim \int_{t_S}^{t_O} dt\, a^{-3}$, while leaving the standard redshift–scale factor relation $1+z = 1/a$ intact.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 由于自旋守恒，torsion scalar自然满足 $Φ \sim a^{-3}$，无需人为假设；(2) 在非相互作用、以Hubble半径为截断的HDE模型中，torsion将有效状态方程从 $ω_X = 0$ 移至负值，从而实现宇宙加速；(3) 即使在弱torsion区域（$|Φ/H| < 1$），状态方程也可趋近 $ω_X \simeq -1$ 并穿越phantom分界线（$ω_X < -1$）；(4) 模型预言暗能量动力学导致宇宙加速逐渐减弱，可能与DESI观测一致；(5) torsion修改了宇宙距离对偶关系，引入偏离参数 $η \sim \int_{t_S}^{t_O} dt\, a^{-3}$，但保持标准红移–尺度因子关系 $1+z = 1/a$ 不变。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that Einstein-Cartan gravity with a Weyssenhoff fluid provides a theoretically consistent mechanism to realize viable holographic dark energy with the Hubble radius as the infrared cutoff. Torsion, arising naturally from spin density, resolves the ansatz problem and enables late-time cosmic acceleration without requiring dark sector interactions. The resulting model is phenomenologically promising—featuring phantom crossing, weakening acceleration, and testable modifications to distance relations—thus offering a robust framework for future observational tests and likelihood analyses in cosmology.
\n\n
本文表明，结合Weyssenhoff流体的Einstein-Cartan引力为实现以Hubble半径为红外截断的可行holographic dark energy提供了理论自洽的机制。由自旋密度自然产生的torsion解决了ansatz问题，并在无需暗区相互作用的情况下实现晚期宇宙加速。所得模型具有可观测前景：包含phantom穿越、加速减弱效应以及可检验的距离关系修正，为未来宇宙学观测检验和似然分析提供了坚实理论框架。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate non-interacting holographic dark energy (HDE) with the Hubble radius as the infrared cutoff in Einstein-Cartan gravity. We derive the Einstein-Cartan equations from the action principle and obtain Friedmann-like equations by introducing a torsion scalar. Considering a Weyssenhoff spin fluid, we determine the scaling behavior of the torsion scalar as $Φ\sim a^{-3}$ without introducing an ad hoc ansatz, resolving the ansatz problem of previous torsion scalar scenarios. In the absence of interactions between dark matter and dark energy, the torsion scalar shifts the equation of state for holographic dark energy toward negative values from the dust-like value obtained in HDE without torsion, making cosmic acceleration possible. In particular, the resulting equation of state can approach $ω_X \simeq -1$ and cross the phantom divide within the weak torsion regime $|Φ/H| < 1$. The model predicts a dynamical equation of state in which cosmic acceleration gradually weakens, potentially consistent with recent DESI observations. In spacetimes with torsion, the cosmic distance duality relation between the luminosity distance $d_L$ and the angular diameter distance $d_A$ is modified as $d_L = d_A (1+z)^2 (1+η)$. In the presence of the torsion scalar, we show that the standard relation between redshift and the scale factor is preserved, while the deviation parameter arising from torsion effects is determined as $η\sim \int_{t_S}^{t_O} dt a^{-3}$, where $t_S$ and $t_O$ denote the emission time at the source and the observation time at the observer, respectively. Overall, our results support the feasibility of the model and provide a theoretical framework for preparing likelihood analyses.

</details>


### [22] [Landauer entropy of spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22172)
*J. M. Isidro,B. Koch,A. Rincon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper proposes a geometric definition of entropy for static, spherically symmetric spacetimes using Landauer's principle, by integrating the entropy of individual geodesics over a surface. It proves a second law for this "Landauer entropy" and connects it to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy.
\n\n
本文基于Landauer原理，为静态球对称时空提出了一种几何熵定义：通过对表面上测地线的熵进行面积分得到测地线汇的熵。在若干温和假设下，作者建立了该“Landauer熵”的第二定律，并将其与Bekenstein-Hawking熵联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from seeking a microscopic or information-theoretic origin of gravitational entropy, particularly the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. By invoking Landauer's principle—which links information erasure to thermodynamic entropy—the authors aim to ground spacetime entropy in the behavior of geodesic congruences, offering a geometric and operational interpretation.
\n\n
该工作的动机源于探寻引力熵（尤其是Bekenstein-Hawking熵）的微观或信息论起源。通过引入Landauer原理（将信息擦除与热力学熵关联），作者试图将时空熵建立在测地线汇的行为之上，从而提供一种几何且可操作的诠释。

Method: The method involves defining the entropy of a single geodesic via Landauer's principle, then constructing the total entropy of a congruence as the surface integral $S = \\int_{\\Sigma} s\\, dA$, where $s$ is the entropy per geodesic crossing the surface $\\Sigma$. The authors analyze the focusing properties of null or timelike geodesic congruences under mild energy conditions and use Raychaudhuri-type equations to demonstrate monotonicity of this entropy along the flow, establishing a second law.
\n\n
该方法首先通过Landauer原理定义单条测地线的熵，然后将测地线汇的总熵构造为面积分 $S = \\int_{\\Sigma} s\\, dA$，其中 $s$ 是穿过表面 $\\Sigma$ 的每条测地线所携带的熵。作者在温和的能量条件下分析零或类时测地线汇的聚焦性质，并利用Raychaudhuri型方程证明该熵沿流单调不减，从而建立第二定律。

Result: The paper shows that the defined Landauer entropy satisfies a classical second law—i.e., it is non-decreasing under geodesic evolution—and that for black hole horizons, this entropy reproduces the Bekenstein-Hawking formula $S_{\\text{BH}} = A/(4G\\hbar)$ up to a proportionality constant fixed by Landauer’s bound.
\n\n
论文表明，所定义的Landauer熵满足经典第二定律——即在测地线演化下不减；并且对于黑洞视界，该熵重现了Bekenstein-Hawking公式 $S_{\\text{BH}} = A/(4G\\hbar)$，其比例常数由Landauer极限确定。

Conclusion: The work provides a novel link between information theory (via Landauer's principle), geometric optics of geodesic flows, and black hole thermodynamics, suggesting that gravitational entropy may emerge from the information cost of tracking geodesic trajectories across horizons.
\n\n
本工作通过Landauer原理，在信息论、测地线流的几何光学与黑洞热力学之间建立了新颖联系，表明引力熵可能源于跨越视界追踪测地线轨迹所需的信息代价。

Abstract: Based on Landauer's principle, we provide a geometrical definition for the entropy of a given static, spherically symmetric spacetime. Considering a congruence of geodesics across a surface, one defines the entropy of a congruence as the surface integral of the entropy of the constituent geodesics. Under certain mild assumptions, we establish a second law for the entropy function thus defined (Landauer entropy), and relate it to Bekenstein-Hawking entropy.

</details>


### [23] [Dark Energy in Ghost-free non-local Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22348)
*S. D. Odintsov,V. K. Oikonomou,G. S. Sharov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Ghost-free non-local gravity matches $\\Lambda$CDM and generalized exponential $F(R)$ gravity when tested against Pantheon+, DESI, and $H(z)$ data alone, but fails to remain competitive once CMB data are included. The generalized exponential $F(R)$ model outperforms both $\\Lambda$CDM and non-local gravity in full-data statistical tests due to its dynamical dark energy equation of state transitioning from phantom to quintessence.
\n\n
Ghost-free non-local gravity 在仅使用 Pantheon+、DESI 和 $H(z)$ 数据时与 $\\Lambda$CDM 和广义指数型 $F(R)$ 引力模型表现相当，但在加入 CMB 数据后表现不佳。广义指数型 $F(R)$ 模型因其有效暗能量状态方程在晚期宇宙中从 phantom 相演化到 quintessence 相，在包含全部观测数据（含 CMB）的统计检验中优于 $\\Lambda$CDM 和 non-local gravity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to assess the viability of ghost-free non-local gravity as an alternative to $\\Lambda$CDM by confronting it with modern cosmological observations. A key motivation is to understand whether such infrared-modified gravity models can explain late-time cosmic acceleration without invoking dark energy, while remaining consistent across diverse datasets including supernovae, BAO, $H(z)$, DESI, and CMB.
\n\n
本文旨在通过将 ghost-free non-local gravity 与现代宇宙学观测数据进行对比，评估其作为 $\\Lambda$CDM 替代方案的可行性。核心动机是探究这类红外修正引力模型能否在不引入暗能量的前提下解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀，并在包括超新星、BAO、$H(z)$、DESI 和 CMB 在内的多种观测数据集中保持一致性。

Method: The authors perform observational constraints on ghost-free non-local gravity and generalized exponential $F(R)$ gravity using a joint analysis of multiple datasets: the Pantheon+ Type Ia supernovae sample, Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) BAO measurements, cosmic chronometer $H(z)$ data, and Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) observations. They compare the models via $\\chi^2$ statistics and information criteria (e.g., AIC, BIC), focusing on the effective dark energy equation of state $w_{\\text{eff}}(z)$ derived from the modified Friedmann equations.
\n\n
作者通过对多个数据集的联合分析对 ghost-free non-local gravity 和广义指数型 $F(R)$ 引力模型进行观测约束：包括 Pantheon+ Ia 型超新星样本、Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) 的 BAO 测量、宇宙计时器 $H(z)$ 数据以及宇宙微波背景（CMB）观测。他们通过 $\\chi^2$ 统计量和信息准则（如 AIC、BIC）比较模型，并聚焦于由修正 Friedmann 方程导出的有效暗能量状态方程 $w_{\\text{eff}}(z)$。

Result: Ghost-free non-local gravity provides a good fit to Pantheon+, DESI, and $H(z)$ data, performing comparably to $\\Lambda$CDM and generalized exponential $F(R)$ gravity. However, when CMB data are added, its fit deteriorates significantly. In contrast, the generalized exponential $F(R)$ model not only satisfies theoretical viability conditions (e.g., stability, absence of ghosts) but also achieves a better $\\chi^2$ and lower information criteria than $\\Lambda$CDM across the full dataset. This advantage stems from its effective equation of state $w_{\\text{eff}}(z)$ evolving from $w < -1$ (phantom) at higher redshifts to $w > -1$ (quintessence) at low redshifts, offering richer late-time dynamics than the purely quintessence-like behavior ($w > -1$ at all times) of non-local gravity.

Conclusion: While ghost-free non-local gravity remains a viable candidate for explaining late-time cosmic acceleration when restricted to low-redshift probes (Pantheon+, DESI, $H(z)$), it fails to maintain consistency with high-redshift CMB data. The generalized exponential $F(R)$ gravity emerges as a more robust alternative, surpassing $\\Lambda$CDM in statistical measures and offering a dynamically evolving dark energy behavior that better accommodates the full range of cosmological observations.
\n\n
尽管 ghost-free non-local gravity 在仅限低红移探针（Pantheon+、DESI、$H(z)$）时仍是解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀的可行候选者，但它无法与高红移 CMB 数据保持一致。广义指数型 $F(R)$ 引力则展现出更强的鲁棒性，在统计指标上超越 $\\Lambda$CDM，并提供动态演化的暗能量行为，能更好地兼容全范围的宇宙学观测数据。

Abstract: Ghost-free non-local gravity is investigated with regards to its late-time dynamics. Viable solutions in this model are confronted with the observational data including the Pantheon+ catalogue of Type Ia supernovae, the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument, the measurements of baryon acoustic oscillations and the Hubble parameter estimations $H(z)$. The ghost-free non-local gravity is found to be successful in these tests in comparison to the $Λ$CDM model and can be also comparable with the generalized exponential $F(R)$ gravity scenario. However the model encounters difficulties when the data from the above observations and the cosmic microwave background radiation data are combined. In tests with the whole set of Pantheon+, DESI, $H(z)$ and CMB data, the generalized exponential $F(R)$ model is essentially more successful. This success is related with the dynamical behavior of its effective dark energy equation of state evolving from a phantom to a quintessence phase during the late-time epoch, whereas the ghost-free non-local model demonstrates only a quintessence behavior. Hence the ghost-free non-local gravity scenario is successful only when the Pantheon+, DESI and $H(z)$ data are considered. The generalized exponential $F(R)$ model satisfies the viability conditions and in tests with all observational data including CMB surpasses the $Λ$CDM model in $χ^2$ statistics and also with information criteria.

</details>

Using Mino-time and elliptic integrals, the authors obtain closed-form solutions for particle orbits, revealing divergent behavior in coordinate time and azimuthal angle near degenerate throats, while regular transit occurs for simple-root throats. Homoclinic orbits and Lyapunov exponents are computed, offering potential observational discriminators from Schwarzschild black holes.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The primary motivation is to explore whether Damour-Solodukhin wormholes—phenomenological alternatives to black holes that mimic Schwarzschild geometry externally but possess a traversable throat—can be observationally distinguished via geodesic dynamics. Specifically, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) in such wormholes may differ fundamentally from that in Schwarzschild spacetime due to the presence of the throat and its interplay with degeneracies in the radial potential. Understanding these differences through exact analytical solutions provides a theoretical foundation for future gravitational-wave or accretion-disk observations.
\n\n
主要动机是探究Damour-Solodukhin虫洞——一类在外部模仿Schwarzschild几何但具有可穿越喉部的黑洞替代理论模型——是否能通过测地线动力学被观测区分。特别是，此类虫洞中的最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）可能因喉部存在及其与径向势多重根的相互作用而与Schwarzschild时空中的ISCO有本质不同。通过精确解析解理解这些差异，可为未来的引力波或吸积盘观测提供理论基础。

Method: The authors employ the Mino-time parametrization to decouple the radial and angular equations of motion for timelike geodesics in the Damour-Solodukhin metric. The radial effective potential is analyzed algebraically, revealing four real roots, one of which is fixed at the throat radius $r_{\rm th} = 2M\\sqrt{1 + λ^2}$. Degenerate cases (double, triple, quartic roots) are identified by solving discriminant conditions. Particle trajectories are expressed in terms of incomplete elliptic integrals of the first, second, and third kinds. For degenerate roots located at the throat, asymptotic expansions yield logarithmic or power-law divergences in coordinate time $t$ and azimuthal angle $φ$. Homoclinic orbits are constructed as separatrices between bound and plunging orbits, and the Lyapunov exponent is derived from the linearized radial equation near unstable circular orbits.
\n\n
作者采用Mino时间参数化方法，将Damour-Solodukhin度规中类时测地线的径向与角向运动方程解耦。通过对径向有效势进行代数分析，发现其具有四个实根，其中之一固定在喉部半径$r_{\rm th} = 2M\\sqrt{1 + λ^2}$处。通过求解判别式条件识别出多重根情形（二重、三重、四重根）。粒子轨迹用第一、第二和第三类不完全椭圆积分表示。当多重根位于喉部时，通过渐近展开得到坐标时间$t$和方位角$φ$的对数或幂律发散行为。同宿轨道被构造为束缚轨道与坠入轨道之间的分界线，并通过对不稳定圆轨道附近的径向方程线性化导出Lyapunov指数。

Result: Key results include: (1) analytic expressions for $r(τ)$, $φ(λ)$, and $t(λ)$ in terms of elliptic integrals for generic orbits; (2) identification of the ISCO with a triple-root configuration where the throat coincides with two other potential roots; (3) demonstration that when the throat is a multiple root, $t$ and $φ$ diverge as a particle approaches it, preventing smooth traversal; (4) in contrast, if the throat is a simple root, particles cross it regularly, connecting the two asymptotically flat universes; (5) exact homoclinic solutions are derived, and the associated Lyapunov exponent is computed as $γ = \\sqrt{-V''_{\\rm eff}(r_{\\rm isco})}/2$, characterizing orbital instability; (6) inspiral and plunge trajectories through the throat are explicitly constructed, showing distinct signatures from black hole counterparts.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 对一般轨道给出了用椭圆积分表示的$r(τ)$、$φ(λ)$和$t(λ)$的解析表达式；(2) 将ISCO识别为一种三重根构型，其中喉部与势函数的另外两个根重合；(3) 证明当喉部为多重根时，粒子趋近喉部过程中$t$和$φ$发散，无法平滑穿越；(4) 相反，若喉部为单根，粒子可规则穿越，连接两个渐近平直宇宙；(5) 推导出精确的同宿解，并计算相应的Lyapunov指数为$γ = \\sqrt{-V''_{\\rm eff}(r_{\\rm isco})}/2$，刻画轨道不稳定性；(6) 显式构造了穿过喉部的旋进与坠入轨迹，显示出与黑洞情形不同的特征。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that Damour-Solodukhin wormholes exhibit rich geodesic structure governed by the interplay between the throat and degeneracies of the radial potential. The analytic solutions reveal qualitative differences from Schwarzschild black holes—especially in ISCO location, transit behavior through the throat, and divergence properties of observables—which could serve as smoking-gun signatures in electromagnetic or gravitational-wave observations. The framework developed here provides a robust toolkit for modeling particle and photon dynamics in exotic compact objects beyond black holes.
\n\n
本研究表明，Damour-Solodukhin虫洞的测地线结构由喉部与径向势多重根之间的相互作用所主导。解析解揭示了其与Schwarzschild黑洞的定性差异——特别是在ISCO位置、穿越喉部的行为以及可观测量的发散特性方面——这些差异可能成为电磁或引力波观测中的“确凿证据”。本文建立的框架为建模黑洞之外的奇异致密天体中的粒子与光子动力学提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: We investigate timelike geodesics around Damour-Solodukhin wormholes, which are Schwarzschild-like geometries characterized by a deformation parameter $λ$ that determines the radius of the throat, $r_{\rm th}$. The radial potential admits four roots, including the throat radius itself, allowing the throat to merge with other roots and form double, triple, and quartic degeneracies. In particular, triple-root configurations associated with the throat determine the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), providing a potential observational distinction from Schwarzschild black holes. Using the Mino-time parametrization, we derive particle trajectories with closed-form analytical solutions in terms of incomplete elliptic integrals for both bound and unbound motion. In particular, we focus on double or triple roots are located at the throat, the azimuthal angle and coordinate time exhibit logarithmic or power-law divergences as the particle approaches the throat. By contrast, trajectories remain regular when the throat corresponds to a simple root, allowing particles to traverse smoothly between the two asymptotically flat regions. We also derive exact homoclinic solutions associated with the throat and compute the corresponding Lyapunov exponent. In addition, inspiral and plunge trajectories through the throat are analyzed. These results provide analytic insights into particle dynamics and possible observational signatures of the wormholes.

</details>

Motivation: The motivation stems from exploring how quantum-gravity-inspired Weyl corrections—encoding non-minimal coupling between spacetime curvature and electromagnetism—affect both the theoretical structure (thermodynamics, topology) and observational signatures (photon orbits, shadows, accretion disk emission) of charged black holes, thereby bridging effective field theory with potential astrophysical tests.

该工作的动机在于探索受量子引力启发的Weyl修正（编码时空曲率与电磁场的非最小耦合）如何影响带电黑洞的理论结构（热力学、拓扑）和观测信号（光子轨道、阴影、吸积盘辐射），从而连接有效场论与潜在的天体物理检验。

Method: The authors employ a multi-pronged approach: (1) analytical derivation of thermodynamic quantities ($T_H$, $S$, $C_Q$) from the corrected black hole metric; (2) topological analysis using the winding number method applied to the thermodynamic vector field; (3) null geodesic analysis for two photon polarizations in the Weyl-corrected spacetime to compute black hole shadow and constrain parameters; (4) relativistic thin-disk accretion modeling to compute energy flux $F(r)$, spectral luminosity $L_\\nu$, and differential luminosity $dL_\\nu/d\\ln\\nu$.

作者采用多管齐下的方法：(1) 从修正后的黑洞度规解析推导热力学量（$T_H$、$S$、$C_Q$）；(2) 对热力学矢量场应用winding number方法进行拓扑分析；(3) 在Weyl修正时空中对两种光子偏振进行零测地线分析，计算黑洞阴影并约束参数；(4) 建立相对论性薄盘吸积模型，计算能量通量 $F(r)$、谱光度 $L_\\nu$ 和微分光度 $dL_\\nu/d\\ln\\nu$。

Result: Key results include: modified expressions for Hawking temperature and entropy showing explicit dependence on the Weyl parameter $\\gamma$; altered heat capacity indicating shifted thermal stability regimes and phase transition points; topological classification revealing how $\\gamma$ affects the universal winding number; distinct photon spheres and shadow shapes for different polarizations; and quantifiable deviations in accretion disk spectra (e.g., peak frequency shifts and luminosity changes) attributable to Weyl corrections.

Conclusion: Weyl corrections significantly alter both the intrinsic thermodynamic/topological properties and extrinsic observational features of charged black holes. These modifications provide concrete theoretical predictions that could be tested through high-resolution black hole shadow imaging (e.g., by EHT) and precision spectroscopy of accretion disks, offering a potential observational window into curvature-electromagnetism couplings beyond standard General Relativity.

Abstract: In this work, we delve into the physics of charged black holes modified by Weyl corrections, a framework that emerges from the subtle non--minimal coupling between spacetime curvature and electromagnetism. We begin by revisiting the thermodynamics of these cases, where we derive the Hawking temperature, entropy, and heat capacity to see how the Weyl correction parameter reshapes the landscape of thermal stability and phase transitions. Then, we apply the winding number method to classify the thermodynamic states of the system from a topological perspective and show the effect of the Weyl modifications on the universal classification of the Wey--corrected black hole. Moving beyond pure theory and into the realm of astrophysics, we study the motion of massless particles affected by the Weyl correction for the two photon polarization, and by exploring the shadow, we find constraints of the black hole parameters. Also, we study the null trajectories for the two photon polarization of the Weyl--corrected black hole. Finally, we model the accretion disk around these black holes. By calculating the energy flux, spectral luminosity, and differential luminosity, we show how these corrections leave a detectable trace on the light we might observe.

This theoretical prediction is then compared with observational data from Do et al. (2019) and Gillessen et al. (2017) using a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) method to statistically constrain the three halo parameters: the inner slope $\gamma$, the scale density $\rho_s$, and the scale radius $r_s$.

作者采用一个新近推导出的广义Schwarzschild度规，其中包含具有任意$\gamma$的球对称Dehnen型$(1,4,\gamma)$暗物质晕。他们推导了在此时空中测试粒子（代表S2恒星）的测地线运动方程，并计算了一个轨道周期内的相对论性近心点进动。随后，将该理论预言与Do et al. (2019)和Gillessen et al. (2017)的观测数据进行比较，利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法对三个晕参数——内斜率$\gamma$、标度密度$\rho_s$和标度半径$r_s$——进行统计约束。

Result: Using MCMC analysis, the best-fit parameters for the Dehnen halo are found to be $\gamma = 1.18^{+1.03}_{-0.81}$, $\rho_s = 0.37^{+0.42}_{-0.29}$, and $r_s = 0.05^{+0.05}_{-0.03}$ (in suitable units) based on Do et al.'s data, and $\gamma = 1.23^{+1.01}_{-0.85}$, $\rho_s = 0.31^{+0.44}_{-0.26}$, $r_s = 0.14^{+0.18}_{-0.10}$ from Gillessen et al.'s data. Conclusion: Precise tracking of stellar orbits like that of S2 provides a powerful tool to probe the dark matter distribution in the immediate vicinity of supermassive black holes. The generalized Schwarzschild–Dehnen framework allows for robust constraints on the inner slope $\gamma$ and other halo parameters, demonstrating that current observational data already limit the possible DM density profiles around Sgr A$^{\star}$. Future higher-precision measurements could further distinguish between cored, cuspy, or more exotic DM configurations.

对S2等恒星轨道的精确追踪是探测超大质量黑洞邻近区域暗物质分布的有力工具。广义Schwarzschild–Dehnen框架使得对内斜率$\gamma$及其他晕参数的稳健限制成为可能，表明当前观测数据已能有效限制Sgr A$^{\star}$周围的暗物质密度轮廓。未来更高精度的测量有望进一步区分平核、尖点或更奇特的暗物质构型。

Abstract: The distribution of dark matter (DM) halo around supermassive black holes (BHs) may leave observable imprints on stellar dynamics near galactic centers. Motivated by this, we investigate the orbital motion of the S2 star in the spacetime of a recently derived generalized Schwarzschild BH solution embedded in a Dehnen-type $(1,4,γ)$ DM halo, considering it as a possible model for Sgr A$^{\star}$ at the center of the Milky Way. Unlike previous studies restricted to specific values of the halo parameter $γ$, the present solution describes the fully generalized case with arbitrary $γ$. We derive the corresponding equations of motion and obtain the associated perihelion shift over one orbital period. Using observational data of the S2 star, we constrain the parameters of the Schwarzschild--Dehnen BH-DM system through a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis. Our results yield the best-fit values $γ= 1.18^{+1.03}_{-0.81}$ $(1.23^{+1.01}_{-0.85})$, $ρ_s = 0.37^{+0.42}_{-0.29}$ $(0.31^{+0.44}_{-0.26})$, and $r_s = 0.05^{+0.05}_{-0.03}$ $(0.14^{+0.18}_{-0.10})$ for observational data of Do et al.~\cite{Do19} and Gillessen et al.~\cite{Gillessen17ApJ}, respectively. We further obtain the corresponding 95\% confidence upper bounds: $γ< 2.66$ $(2.67)$, $ρ_s < 0.93$ $(0.92)$, and $r_s < 0.16$ $(0.52)$. These results demonstrate that precise stellar orbit measurements can provide meaningful constraints on the DM halo distributions surrounding supermassive BHs and may offer insights into the DM environment of Sgr A$^{\star}$ at the center of the Milky Way.

</details>


### [27] [Vector modes in Type 3 New GR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22329)

作者聚焦于最简单的非平凡情形：Type 3 New GR中的矢量扰动。他们显式推导出二次拉格朗日量及其对应的线性化运动方程。通过比较两种方法，表明将约束方程（并非独立的动力学方程）代入拉格朗日量会人为减少约束数量，从而错误地将非动力学变量提升为动力学变量。正确的自由度计数必须在运动方程层面进行，或通过对拉格朗日量进行Dirac–Bergmann约束分析（且不预先代入约束）来完成。

Conclusion: The paper reaffirms that the correct method to count physical degrees of freedom in gauge theories like New GR is to analyze the full set of linearized equations of motion or apply a proper Dirac–Bergmann algorithm to the Lagrangian without eliminating variables using constraints prematurely. 本文重申，在New GR等规范理论中，正确计算物理自由度的方法是分析完整的线性化运动方程组，或对拉格朗日量应用恰当的Dirac–Bergmann算法（而不提前用约束消去变量）。将约束代入拉格朗日量会扭曲约束结构，导致对动力学模的错误识别。这解决了争议，并维护了原始的自由度计数结果。

Abstract: Some time ago, we published the full count of degrees of freedom in the linearised weak gravity limit of arbitrary New GR models. We did it by considering the linear equations of motion and presented a thorough analysis with no ambiguity left. A bit later, we generalised it to linear cosmological perturbations and discussed the strong coupling issues that appear already at this level. Recently, there were claims that some dynamical modes had been missed in our work. However, the authors of the new claims did not look at the equations of motion and analysed the quadratic Lagrangian densities instead. In this paper, I take one of the most elementary cases, namely the vector modes in New GR of Type 3, and show what was their mistake that had led them to claiming that those were dynamical. The main message: Do not substitute constraint equations into a Lagrangian.

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper investigates black hole entropy in diffeomorphism-invariant gravity theories with nonminimal matter–curvature couplings, showing that the standard Wald entropy $S_{\mathrm W}$ may be insufficient when matter fields are singular at the bifurcation surface.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In diffeomorphism-invariant gravitational theories with nonminimal couplings between matter and curvature, the standard Wald entropy formula—derived via the Iyer–Wald Noether charge method—assumes smoothness of all fields at the bifurcation surface of a Killing horizon. However, when matter fields cannot be smoothly extended to this surface (e.g., due to singular or non-analytic behavior), the horizon surface charge variation may contain additional finite contributions not captured by the Wald entropy density. This motivates a systematic decomposition of the full entropy entering the first law of black hole mechanics to determine when and how the Wald formula must be corrected.

在物质与曲率存在非极小耦合的微分同胚不变引力理论中，标准Wald熵公式（通过Iyer–Wald Noether荷方法导出）假设所有场在Killing视界的分岔面上光滑。然而，当物质场无法光滑延拓至该面（例如由于奇异或非解析行为）时，视界表面荷变分可能包含Wald熵密度未涵盖的额外有限贡献。这促使作者系统地分解进入黑洞力学第一定律的完整熵，以确定Wald公式何时以及如何需要修正。

Method: The authors employ the covariant phase space formalism to compute the variation of the horizon surface charge associated with the horizon-generating Killing vector. Starting from the action varied directly, they subtract standard work terms (e.g., variations of conserved charges like mass or angular momentum) and isolate the integrable part of the surface charge variation that contributes to entropy. They then decompose this entropy into three components: the Wald entropy $S_{\mathrm W}$ from the Iyer–Wald Noether charge, a non-Wald Noether charge contribution $S_1$, and an additional integrable piece $ΔS$ from the surface charge variation not captured by the Noether charge. This decomposition is applied to specific models: Kalb–Ramond theory, bumblebee gravity, and Weyl-vector extended Gauss–Bonnet gravity.
\n\n
作者采用协变相空间形式（covariant phase space formalism）计算与视界生成Killing矢量相关的视界表面荷变分。从直接变分作用量出发，扣除标准功项（如质量或角动量等守恒荷的变分），分离出对熵有贡献的表面荷变分中的可积部分。随后将该熵分解为三部分：来自Iyer–Wald Noether荷的Wald熵 $S_{\mathrm W}$、Noether荷的非Wald贡献 $S_1$，以及表面荷变分中未被Noether荷捕获的额外可积项 $ΔS$。此分解被应用于具体模型：Kalb–Ramond理论、bumblebee引力和Weyl矢量扩展的Gauss–Bonnet引力。

Result: For regular Kalb–Ramond black holes, the total horizon entropy equals the Wald entropy ($S_{\mathrm H}=S_{\mathrm W}$), indicating no correction is needed. In contrast, Weyl-vector extended Gauss–Bonnet black holes require both $S_1$ and $ΔS$ to correctly reproduce the thermodynamic entropy, demonstrating that the Wald formula alone is insufficient. These results provide a concrete criterion: if matter fields are singular at the bifurcation surface, the full surface charge variation—not just the Wald density—must be used to obtain the correct entropy in the first law.

Conclusion: The Wald entropy formula, while robust in many contexts, is not universally sufficient for black hole thermodynamics in theories with nonminimal matter–curvature couplings. When matter fields fail to be smooth at the bifurcation surface, the horizon entropy receives additional contributions from the surface charge variation that are not encoded in the Noether charge density. The decomposition $S_{\mathrm H}=S_{\mathrm W}+S_1+ΔS$ provides a systematic framework to identify and compute these corrections. This work establishes a direct diagnostic tool—based on the behavior of matter fields at the horizon—for determining whether the full covariant phase space surface charge must be used instead of the Wald entropy alone.
\n\n
Wald熵公式虽在许多情形下稳健，但在具有非极小物质–曲率耦合的理论中并非普适适用于黑洞热力学。当物质场在分岔面不光滑时，视界熵会从表面荷变分中获得Noether荷密度未编码的额外贡献。分解式 $S_{\mathrm H}=S_{\mathrm W}+S_1+ΔS$ 提供了一个系统框架来识别和计算这些修正。本工作建立了一个直接的诊断工具——基于物质场在视界处的行为——用于判断是否必须使用完整的协变相空间表面荷，而非仅依赖Wald熵。

Abstract: We study the entropy of static, spherically symmetric black holes in diffeomorphism-invariant theories with nonminimal matter--curvature couplings, using the covariant phase space formalism. For regular bifurcate Killing horizons, the Iyer--Wald construction gives the standard Wald entropy. If a matter field cannot be smoothly extended to the regular bifurcation surface, however, the horizon surface charge variation can contain finite contributions that are not included in the Wald entropy density. In the representative obtained by directly varying the action, and after ordinary work terms are subtracted, we decompose the entropy entering the first law of black hole thermodynamics as \(S_{\mathrm H}=S_{\mathrm W}+S_1+ΔS\). Here \(S_{\mathrm W}\) is the Wald entropy, \(S_1\) is the non-Wald part of the Noether charge, and \(ΔS\) is the remaining integrable part of the horizon surface charge variation. Applying this criterion to Kalb--Ramond, bumblebee, and extended Gauss--Bonnet black holes, we find that the regular Kalb--Ramond branch has \(S_{\mathrm H}=S_{\mathrm W}\), the bumblebee branches yield either \(S_1=0\) with \(ΔS\neq0\) or a cancellation between \(S_1\) and \(ΔS\), and the Weyl-vector extended Gauss--Bonnet examples require both corrections. This gives a direct test of whether the Wald entropy density is sufficient, or whether the full horizon surface charge variation has to be used.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the desire to understand gravity as an emergent phenomenon through thermodynamic or entropic principles, as initiated by Padmanabhan’s entropy functional. Method: The authors employ Padmanabhan’s entropy density $s = -\\frac{1}{8\\pi G}(\\nabla_a u_b)(\\nabla^a u^b)$, where $u^a$ is the normal to the foliation, and treat it as a Lagrangian density. By performing a variational principle with respect to the normal vector field $u^a$, they identify the canonical momentum $\\pi^{ab} = \\delta s / \\delta(\\nabla_a u_b)$. The Brown-York tensor is then obtained as the projection of this momentum onto the hypersurface: $T_{ij}^{\\text{BY}} = h_i^a h_j^b \\pi_{ab}$, where $h_{ab}$ is the induced metric. This method is applied to both timelike and null hypersurfaces using appropriate projectors. The framework is extended to scalar-tensor theories by incorporating a non-minimally coupled scalar field $\\phi$ into the entropy functional, leading to modified momentum and boundary tensors.

分析成功地从熵泛函直接导出了类时边界的标准Brown-York张量及其在类光边界上的适当推广，并解释了类光BY张量非对称性的根源——源于诱导度规的退化性及投影算子的结构。在作用量为 $S = \\int d^4x \\sqrt{-g} \\left[ \\frac{1}{16\\pi G} F(\\phi) R - \\frac{1}{2}(\\nabla \\phi)^2 - V(\\phi) \\right]$ 的标量-张量理论中，基于熵的变分原理复现了修正的Einstein方程及相应的BY张量，并表明其满足 $D^i T_{ij}^{\\text{BY}} \\propto \\partial_j \\phi$，从而明确其非守恒性直接源于标量场的非最小耦合。

Abstract: Building on Padmanabhan's entropy functional, originally introduced to derive Einstein's equations and highlight the emergent nature of gravity, we demonstrate its robustness in a broader context. Using the same entropy density, we show that the Brown-York (BY) tensor arises naturally as the projection of the canonical momentum conjugate to the normal vectors on the relevant hypersurface, thereby providing a common construction applicable to both timelike and null hypersurfaces. This perspective also offers insight into the structural differences of the null BY tensor, including its non-symmetric character. We further extend the analysis to scalar-tensor theories, showing that the entropy-based formulation reproduces the expected equations of motion along with the corresponding BY tensor, and, clarifies its non-conservation in the presence of additional scalar field which is non-minimally coupled. The winding number (or topological charge) around each zero is computed via contour integration: $W = \\frac{1}{2\\pi} \\oint d\\phi$, where $\\phi = \\arctan(V^t / V^r)$. This topological invariant classifies thermodynamic branches and identifies critical points where the winding number changes or multiple zeros coalesce.

Abstract: We investigate the weak-field regime of generalized hybrid metric-Palatini theories, described by a generic function \(f(R,\mathcal{R})\), where \(R\) is the metric Ricci scalar and \(\mathcal{R}\) is constructed from an independent torsionless connection. The absence of tachyonic and ghostlike instabilities at the linearized level, together with the nondegeneracy of the scalar sector, is shown to impose algebraic restrictions on the derivatives of \(f(R,\mathcal R)\) evaluated on the Minkowski background, which generalize previously obtained conditions. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard MC@NLO simulations produce a fraction of events with negative weights (from H events), which increases statistical uncertainties and computational costs in Monte Carlo integration. Method: Starting from a standard MC@NLO sample for $pp \\to ZZ$, the authors remove all negative-weight H events and compensate by applying a veto on the first parton shower emission of the corresponding S events. This preserves total cross-section and differential distributions up to power-suppressed corrections. The implementation is carried out within the MadGraph5_aMC@NLO + Pythia8 framework.

Result: The MAcNLOPS implementation shows excellent agreement with standard MC@NLO for both inclusive and radiation-sensitive diboson observables, except for a small discrepancy in the very low-$p_T$ region due to power-suppressed terms. All negative H weights are eliminated with negligible additional CPU cost, while negative S weights remain unaffected.

MAcNLOPS 实现与标准 MC@NLO 在包容性和辐射敏感的双玻色子观测量上表现出极佳的一致性，仅在极低 $p_T$ 区域因幂律压低项存在微小偏差。所有负的 H 权重被完全消除，额外计算开销可忽略，而 S 事件中的负权重保持不变。

Abstract: We present an implementation of the MAcNLOPS matching prescription for $pp \to ZZ$ production in a MadGraph5_aMC@NLO + Pythia8 setup. Starting from a standard MC@NLO event sample, negative H events are removed and compensated by a veto applied to the first shower emission of the S events. The implementation is validated against MC@NLO for radiation-sensitive and inclusive diboson observables. Agreement is found up to a rather small power-suppressed contribution affecting the very low-pT region. The method removes all negative H weights with negligible additional computational cost, while negative S weights are left unchanged, showing that MAcNLOPS is a promising alternative to MC@NLO with a reduced fraction of negative weights.

</details>

It bridges hadron resonance gas at low energies, lattice QCD at low baryon chemical potential $\\mu_B$, and extrapolates to high-density regimes probed by experiments like RHIC BES and FAIR. Thermodynamic consistency is ensured via a physics-informed loss function, and the EoS is implemented in a hydrokinetic model with zero net strangeness and fixed electric-to-baryon ratio.

作者利用深度神经网络构建了一个四维状态方程（EoS），适用于强相互作用物质在温度和守恒荷密度空间中的全域。该EoS 在低能区复现强子共振气体（hadron resonance gas）热力学，在低重子化学势 $\\mu_B$ 区与格点QCD（lattice QCD）一致，并外推至RHIC BES、FAIR等实验所探索的高密区域。通过物理信息损失函数（physics-informed loss function）保证整个相空间的热力学一致性，并在净奇异数为零、电荷-重子比固定的条件下将其嵌入集成流体动力学模型（integrated hydrokinetic model）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing theoretical approaches—such as the hadron resonance gas model and lattice QCD—are limited in their domains of applicability: the former works well near particlization temperatures but fails at high densities, while the latter suffers from the sign problem at finite baryon chemical potential. Experiments like RHIC Beam Energy Scan (BES), FAIR, HADES, and CBM aim to probe the high-$\\mu_B$ region of the QCD phase diagram, necessitating a unified, thermodynamically consistent EoS that interpolates between known limits and reliably extrapolates into uncharted territory.
\n\n
现有理论方法——如强子共振气体模型和格点QCD——各自适用范围有限：前者在碎裂（particlization）温度附近表现良好，但在高密区失效；后者在有限重子化学势下受符号问题（sign problem）困扰。RHIC 束流能量扫描（BES）、FAIR、HADES 和 CBM 等实验旨在探测 QCD 相图中高 $\\mu_B$ 区域，因此亟需一个统一且热力学自洽的状态方程（EoS），能够在已知极限之间插值并可靠地外推至未知区域。

Method: A deep neural network is trained to represent the pressure $P(T, \\mu_B, \\mu_Q, \\mu_S)$ as a function of temperature $T$ and chemical potentials for baryon ($\\mu_B$), electric charge ($\\mu_Q$), and strangeness ($\\mu_S$). The training incorporates data from the hadron resonance gas model at low $T$ and lattice QCD at small $\\mu_B/T$. Crucially, thermodynamic identities—such as $n_i = \\partial P / \\partial \\mu_i$ and $s = \\partial P / \\partial T$—are enforced through a physics-informed loss function that penalizes deviations from exact Maxwell relations. The resulting EoS is then implemented in the integrated hydrokinetic model under constraints of zero net strangeness ($n_S = 0$) and fixed $n_Q/n_B$.
\n\n
作者训练一个深度神经网络来表示压强 $P(T, \\mu_B, \\mu_Q, \\mu_S)$，作为温度 $T$ 以及重子（$\\mu_B$）、电荷（$\\mu_Q$）和奇异数（$\\mu_S$）化学势的函数。训练数据包含低温下的强子共振气体模型和小 $\\mu_B/T$ 下的格点QCD结果。关键在于，通过物理信息损失函数强制满足热力学恒等式，例如 $n_i = \\partial P / \\partial \\mu_i$ 和 $s = \\partial P / \\partial T$，并对偏离精确Maxwell关系的行为施加惩罚。最终得到的状态方程在净奇异数为零（$n_S = 0$）和固定电荷-重子数比（$n_Q/n_B$）条件下被嵌入集成流体动力学模型。

Result: The neural network successfully learns a smooth, thermodynamically consistent 4D EoS that matches hadron resonance gas thermodynamics at typical particlization temperatures ($T \\sim 150–160$ MeV), agrees with lattice QCD results up to $\\mu_B/T \\lesssim 2–3$, and provides a physically plausible extrapolation to high baryon densities relevant for future heavy-ion collision experiments. 神经网络成功学习到一个光滑且热力学自洽的四维状态方程，该方程在典型碎裂温度（$T \\sim 150–160$ MeV）下复现强子共振气体热力学，在 $\\mu_B/T \\lesssim 2–3$ 范围内与格点QCD结果一致，并对重离子碰撞实验相关的高重子密度区域给出了物理上合理的外推。基于该EoS的流体动力学模拟在现实约束下可行且稳定。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that physics-informed machine learning can bridge disparate theoretical frameworks and provide a unified, differentiable, and thermodynamically consistent equation of state for QCD matter across its full phase diagram. Such an EoS is essential for interpreting current and future heavy-ion collision data, particularly in the high baryon density regime where first-principles calculations are unavailable.

Abstract: We present a four-dimensional equation of state for strongly interacting matter at finite temperature and conserved charge densities, constructed using a deep neural network. It is designed for direct use in hybrid models of relativistic heavy-ion collisions: it reproduces hadron resonance gas thermodynamics at typical particlization scales, is consistent with lattice QCD at low baryon chemical potential, and extrapolates into the high-density region inaccessible to either approach, which is precisely the regime targeted by RHIC BES, FAIR, HADES, and CBM. Thermodynamic consistency throughout the full phase space is enforced via a physics-informed loss function. We demonstrate the developed equation of state by implementing it at zero net strangeness and fixed electric-to-baryon charge ratio within the integrated hydrokinetic model.

  <summary>Details</summary>

This motivates a detailed study of the resulting enhancement in DM annihilation signals, its thermal implications for NS cooling, and its potential to probe otherwise inaccessible regions of DM parameter space—particularly the neutrino fog regime for direct detection and very small freeze-in annihilation cross-sections.
\n\n
中子星因其极端致密性和强引力场，可在天文时间尺度上通过DM-核子散射高效捕获暗物质粒子，是探测暗物质与可见物质非引力相互作用的理想天体实验室。若暗物质为玻色子，量子统计效应可能导致其在中子星核心形成玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC），从而极大提升局域DM数密度。这促使作者深入研究由此产生的DM湮灭信号增强、对中子星冷却的热效应，以及探测常规手段难以触及的暗物质参数空间（尤其是直接探测中的中微子雾区和极小的freeze-in湮灭截面）的可能性。

Method: The authors compute the capture rate of bosonic DM in neutron stars using standard formalism based on DM-nucleon scattering cross-sections and NS properties (mass, radius, composition). They then model the formation of a Bose-Einstein condensate once the phase-space density exceeds the quantum degeneracy threshold, leading to a macroscopic occupation of the ground state and a corresponding huge increase in number density ($n_{\\text{DM}} \\propto N_{\\text{captured}}$ instead of being limited by thermal pressure). The annihilation rate is calculated as $\\Gamma_{\\text{ann}} = \\langle \\sigma v \\rangle n_{\\text{DM}}^2 V$, where the condensate enhances $n_{\\text{DM}}$ by many orders of magnitude. The resulting energy injection is used to estimate the surface temperature of old neutron stars under steady-state heating, compared against standard cooling curves. They also revisit equilibrium conditions between capture and annihilation, and assess implications for black hole formation from accumulated DM. Finally, they construct a concrete scalar DM model with feeble couplings to the Standard Model that naturally yields the required tiny $\\langle \\sigma v \\rangle$ (freeze-in scale) and DM-nucleon cross-sections.

作者采用基于DM-核子散射截面和中子星性质（质量、半径、成分）的标准形式计算玻色型暗物质在中子星中的捕获率。当相空间密度超过量子简并阈值时，他们建模玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的形成，导致基态宏观占据，从而使数密度大幅增加（$n_{\\text{DM}} \\propto N_{\\text{captured}}$，而非受热压力限制）。湮灭率按$\\Gamma_{\\text{ann}} = \\langle \\sigma v \\rangle n_{\\text{DM}}^2 V$计算，其中凝聚体使$n_{\\text{DM}}$提升多个数量级。由此注入的能量用于估算年老中子星在稳态加热下的表面温度，并与标准冷却曲线对比。他们还重新审视了捕获与湮灭之间的平衡条件，并评估了累积暗物质导致黑洞形成的含义。最后，他们构建了一个具体的标量暗物质模型，其与标准模型的微弱耦合自然产生所需的极小$\\langle \\sigma v \\rangle$（freeze-in量级）和DM-核子散射截面。

Result: The key results are: (1) DM annihilation rate is enhanced by $\\mathcal{O}(10^{15}–10^{20})$ if a BEC forms; (2) even DM with a freeze-in annihilation cross-section ($\\langle \\sigma v \\rangle \\sim 10^{-58}~\\text{cm}^3/\\text{s}$) can heat old neutron stars to temperatures detectable by JWST ($T \\gtrsim 1000~\\text{K}$); (3) this mechanism probes DM-nucleon scattering cross-sections down to $\\sigma_{\\chi n} \\sim 10^{-47}–10^{-45}~\\text{cm}^2$, within the neutrino fog regime inaccessible to terrestrial experiments; (4) the lower bound on the s-wave annihilation cross-section for achieving capture-annihilation equilibrium is significantly relaxed; (5) the critical DM mass for triggering gravitational collapse into a black hole inside the NS is altered due to enhanced annihilation; and (6) a simple scalar DM model with Higgs-portal or similar feeble interactions can naturally realize the required parameter space.

主要结果包括：(1) 若形成BEC，DM湮灭率提升$\\mathcal{O}(10^{15}–10^{20})$倍；(2) 即使具有freeze-in湮灭截面（$\\langle \\sigma v \\rangle \\sim 10^{-58}~\\text{cm}^3/\\text{s}$）的DM也能将年老中子星加热至JWST可探测温度（$T \\gtrsim 1000~\\text{K}$）；(3) 该机制可探测DM-核子散射截面低至$\\sigma_{\\chi n} \\sim 10^{-47}–10^{-45}~\\text{cm}^2$，进入地面实验无法触及的中微子雾区；(4) 实现捕获-湮灭平衡所需的s波湮灭截面下限被显著放宽；(5) 因湮灭增强，触发中子星内部引力坍缩形成黑洞的临界DM质量发生改变；(6) 一个简单的标量DM模型（如Higgs portal或类似微弱耦合机制）可自然实现所需参数空间。

Conclusion: The formation of a Bose-Einstein condensate of captured bosonic dark matter in neutron stars provides a powerful amplification mechanism for annihilation signals, turning old, cold neutron stars into potentially observable infrared sources. This scenario opens a new observational window into extremely weakly interacting dark matter, complementary to terrestrial direct detection, especially in the neutrino fog region. It also revises theoretical constraints related to DM thermalization, equilibrium, and gravitational collapse in compact stars. The feasibility of such signals within well-motivated particle physics models underscores the importance of multi-messenger astrophysical probes in the search for dark matter.

被捕获的玻色型暗物质在中子星内形成玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，为湮灭信号提供了强大的放大机制，可将年老寒冷的中子星转变为潜在可观测的红外源。该情形为探测极弱相互作用暗物质开辟了新的观测窗口，尤其在中微子雾区，可与地面直接探测形成互补。它还修正了关于致密星内暗物质热化、平衡及引力坍缩的相关理论约束。此类信号在有良好动机的粒子物理模型中的可行性，凸显了多信使天体物理探针在暗物质搜寻中的重要性。

Abstract: Neutron star (NS) is one of the most promising astrophysical targets to probe non-gravitational interaction of dark matter (DM) with visible matter. Their compactness makes them an ideal object which can capture particle DM efficiently over its lifetime using the DM-nucleon scattering cross-section. If DM particles are bosonic, then the captured DM population may form a Bose-Einstein condensate at the center of the NS, increasing the DM density significantly. In this work, we study the phenomenology of such scenario with enhanced DM annihilation rate due to the increased density in a condensate. The enhanced DM annihilation makes the NS surface `hotter' than in the standard cooling scenario. We show that the annihilation rate is enhanced by a factor of $\mathcal{O}(10^{15}-10^{20})$ if DM forms a condensate, and DM with freeze-in value annihilation cross-section can heat up the NS to higher temperatures, bringing it within the reach of James Webb Space Telescope. It also allows us to probe DM-nucleon scattering cross section within the neutrino fog regime which will complement the terrestrial direct detection searches. Moreover, the enhanced annihilation from the condensate changes the lower limits on s-wave DM annihilation cross-section for capture-annihilation equilibrium and the formation of a black hole inside the NS. Finally, we show an example of a scalar DM model where such small annihilation and DM-nucleon scattering cross sections can generically arise.

This computational bottleneck prevents real-time feedback from collider data in global analyses, limiting their constraining power and consistency. 对pMSSM等BSM理论进行全局拟合需要同时与多种实验数据（包括直接对撞机限制）进行比对。然而，传统上纳入LHC约束的方法——通常基于完整的探测器模拟或复杂的重新解释框架——在马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）等参数扫描过程中速度过慢。这一计算瓶颈阻碍了对撞机数据在全局分析中的实时反馈，限制了其约束能力和一致性。作者旨在通过构建LHC极限的超快代理模型（surrogates）来克服这一问题。

Method: The authors apply symbolic regression—a machine learning technique that searches for analytic mathematical expressions fitting given input-output data—to a dataset of published ATLAS exclusion limits from electroweakino searches in the pMSSM. The input features are combinations of supersymmetric particle masses and mixing parameters; the output is a binary label (allowed/excluded). The resulting symbolic expression acts as a fast likelihood-like constraint that can be evaluated in microseconds per parameter point, making it suitable for inclusion during global fit scans. This surrogate is then integrated into a standard global fit framework alongside other observables (e.g., dark matter relic density, flavor physics).

作者采用符号回归（symbolic regression）——一种搜索能拟合给定输入-输出数据的解析数学表达式的机器学习技术——处理ATLAS在pMSSM中电弱ino搜寻所发布的排除极限数据集。输入特征是超对称粒子质量与混合参数的组合；输出为二元标签（允许/排除）。所得符号表达式作为一个快速的类似似然（likelihood-like）约束，可在每个参数点微秒级时间内完成评估，适合在全局拟合扫描过程中使用。该代理模型随后被整合进标准全局拟合框架，与其他观测量（如暗物质遗迹密度、味物理）一同使用。

Result: The symbolic regression yields a compact analytic formula that accurately reproduces the ATLAS electroweakino exclusion boundary in the relevant pMSSM subspace. When included in a global fit, this constraint significantly reshapes the posterior probability distribution, excluding regions previously considered viable when LHC limits were applied only a posteriori. The computational overhead per scan point remains negligible (microseconds), enabling fully self-consistent global fits that incorporate LHC Run-2 results in real time.

符号回归生成了一个简洁的解析公式，能够准确复现ATLAS在相关pMSSM子空间中的电弱ino排除边界。当将其纳入全局拟合时，该约束显著重塑了后验概率分布，排除了此前在仅事后（a posteriori）应用LHC极限时被认为可行的参数区域。每次扫描点的计算开销可忽略不计（微秒级），从而实现了完全自洽的全局拟合，可在实时中纳入LHC Run-2结果。

Conclusion: Symbolic regression provides a powerful and efficient pathway to embed complex collider constraints directly into global BSM fits. This approach bridges the gap between high-energy phenomenology and experimental results, enabling more robust, consistent, and computationally feasible global analyses. 符号回归为将复杂的对撞机约束直接嵌入全局BSM拟合提供了一条强大而高效的途径。该方法弥合了高能唯象学与实验结果之间的鸿沟，使得全局分析更加稳健、自洽且计算可行。该方法可推广至其他LHC搜寻及pMSSM以外的BSM场景。

Abstract: Global fits of Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) physics often involve a two-way interplay between theory and experiment. Theoretical models provide guidance for experimental searches, while experimental results, in turn, constrain theoretical frameworks. A crucial aspect of this feedback loop is the direct inclusion of measurements and exclusion limits ``online'' global fits, i.e. during the parameter scans aspects of the global fits. However, incorporating the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) limits into such analyses has been computationally prohibitive, often due to time taken per parameter point exceeding the scales acceptable for global fit frameworks. In this study, we show that LHC limits can be incorporated ``online'' global fits by leveraging approximations derived from symbolic regression techniques. We utilize a dataset of ATLAS constraints from searches for electroweakino productions to derive a mathematical expression capable of classifying the phenomenological Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (pMSSM) parameter space as allowed or excluded. This is subsequently incorporated for making a global fit of the pMSSM to data, including the LHC Run-2 limits.

### [41] [Pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone inflation with $Z_N$ symmetric waterfall fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22464)

TL;DR: This paper proposes a hybrid inflation model where the inflaton is a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson coupled to $N$ waterfall fields with a $Z_N$ symmetry. It demonstrates radiative stability of the inflaton potential due to cancellation of quadratic divergences (for any $N$) and absence of logarithmic divergences (only for $N > 2$). The authors analyze successful inflation parameter space for $Z_2$, $Z_3$, and $Z_4$, study vacuum structure and reheating via Higgs-portal couplings, and show that reheating can occur below the waterfall field mass scale, avoiding domain wall problems. They also suggest multi-component dark matter candidates from $Z_N$ partners of the condensate.

本文提出了一种混合暴胀模型，其中暴胀子为赝Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，并与具有$Z_N$对称性的$N$个瀑布场耦合。研究表明，由于$Z_N$对称性，来自瀑布场的二次发散修正被抵消，而对数发散修正仅在$N>2$时不存在，从而保证了暴胀势的辐射稳定性。作者分析了$Z_2$、$Z_3$和$Z_4$情形下成功暴胀的参数空间，研究了真空结构以及通过$Z_N$不变的Higgs-portal耦合实现的再热过程，并指出再热温度可低于瀑布场凝聚体的质量，从而避免对称性恢复和畴壁问题。此外，文章还探讨了来自瀑布场凝聚体的$Z_N$伙伴构成多组分暗物质的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to construct a radiatively stable hybrid inflation model where the inflaton potential remains protected against large quantum corrections. Standard hybrid inflation models suffer from severe fine-tuning due to quadratically divergent loop corrections from waterfall fields. By promoting the inflaton to a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson and imposing a discrete $Z_N$ symmetry on the waterfall sector, the authors aim to naturally suppress dangerous quantum corrections while enabling rich post-inflationary phenomenology such as safe reheating and dark matter production.
\n\n
动机源于构建一个辐射稳定的混合暴胀模型的需求，其中暴胀势需免受大的量子修正影响。标准混合暴胀模型因瀑布场引起的二次发散圈修正而面临严重的精细调节问题。通过将暴胀子设为赝Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，并在瀑布场部分引入离散$Z_N$对称性，作者旨在自然地抑制有害的量子修正，同时实现丰富的暴胀后现象学，如安全的再热过程和暗物质产生。

Method: The authors employ the Coleman-Weinberg effective potential to compute one-loop corrections to the inflaton potential arising from its coupling to $N$ waterfall scalar fields invariant under $Z_N$ symmetry. They explicitly evaluate quadratic and logarithmic divergences in the cases $N = 2, 3, 4$. The analysis includes solving for slow-roll inflation parameters using the loop-corrected potential, determining viable regions of parameter space, and examining the vacuum manifold after symmetry breaking. Reheating is modeled via $Z_N$-invariant Higgs-portal couplings between waterfall fields and the Standard Model Higgs, and the resulting reheating temperature is compared to the mass scale of the waterfall condensate to assess domain wall formation.
\n\n
作者采用Coleman-Weinberg有效势计算暴胀子与$Z_N$对称性下不变的$N$个瀑布标量场耦合所引起的单圈修正。他们显式计算了$N = 2, 3, 4$情形下的二次和对数发散。分析包括利用圈修正后的势求解慢滚暴胀参数、确定可行参数空间区域，并考察对称性破缺后的真空流形。再热过程通过瀑布场与标准模型Higgs之间的$Z_N$不变Higgs-portal耦合建模，并将所得再热温度与瀑布凝聚体的质量标度比较，以评估畴壁形成的可能性。

Result: The key results are: (1) Quadratically divergent corrections to the inflaton mass cancel for any $N$ due to $Z_N$ symmetry; (2) Logarithmically divergent corrections vanish only when $N > 2$, rendering the inflaton potential radiatively stable in $Z_3$ and $Z_4$ models; (3) Successful inflation is achievable within perturbative couplings for all three cases ($Z_2$, $Z_3$, $Z_4$); (4) Reheating via Higgs-portal interactions can yield $T_{\\text{reh}} < m_{\\phi}$ (waterfall field mass), preventing restoration of $Z_N$ symmetry and thus avoiding the domain wall problem; (5) The $Z_N$-charged remnants of the waterfall condensate may serve as multi-component dark matter candidates.

主要结果包括：(1) 由于$Z_N$对称性，暴胀子质量的二次发散修正在任意$N$下均被抵消；(2) 对数发散修正仅在$N > 2$时消失，使得$Z_3$和$Z_4$模型中的暴胀势具有辐射稳定性；(3) 在所有三种情形（$Z_2$、$Z_3$、$Z_4$）下，均可在微扰耦合范围内实现成功的暴胀；(4) 通过Higgs-portal相互作用的再热过程可导致$T_{\\text{reh}} < m_{\\phi}$（瀑布场质量），防止$Z_N$对称性恢复，从而避免畴壁问题；(5) 瀑布凝聚体的$Z_N$带电残余物可能作为多组分暗物质候选者。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that embedding hybrid inflation in a framework with a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone inflaton and $Z_N$-symmetric waterfall fields provides a natural mechanism for radiative stability, particularly for $N > 2$. The model simultaneously addresses the domain wall problem through low-scale reheating and opens a pathway to multi-component dark matter from symmetry-related states. This approach reconciles theoretical naturalness with observational viability in early-universe cosmology.

文章得出结论：将混合暴胀嵌入到赝Nambu-Goldstone暴胀子与$Z_N$对称瀑布场的框架中，为辐射稳定性（尤其在$N > 2$时）提供了自然机制。该模型通过低能标再热同时解决了畴壁问题，并为来自对称性相关态的多组分暗物质提供了可能路径。这一方法在早期宇宙宇宙学中协调了理论自然性与观测可行性。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid inflation model where a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson inflaton couples to $N$ waterfall scalar fields respecting a $Z_N$ symmetry. We identify the phases for the inflation and the consequent waterfall transition, concretely, in $Z_2$, $Z_3$ and $Z_4$ cases. From the Coleman-Weinberg potential for the inflaton, we show that the quadratically divergent corrections coming from the waterfall sector are cancelled due to the $Z_N$ symmetry, while the logarithmically divergent corrections are absent only for $N>2$, ensuring the radiative stability of the inflaton potential. We show the parameter space for a successful inflation with the loop-corrected inflaton potential in each model and compare the results between different discrete symmetries. We further analyze the vacuum structure of the models and the reheating process due to the $Z_N$-invariant Higgs-portal couplings for the waterfall fields. We find that the reheating temperature can be smaller than the mass of the waterfall field condensate such that the $Z_N$ symmetry is not restored after reheating and there is no domain wall problem in the models. We also comment on the possibility of multi-component dark matter from the $Z_N$ partners of the waterfall field condensate.

</details>

*Longjie Chen,Wolfgang Schäfer,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the production of fully heavy tetraquark states (specifically $T_{4c}(0^{++}, 2^{++})$) in ultraperipheral heavy-ion collisions ($AA \to AA\, J/\psi J/\psi$ and $AA \to AA\, \gamma\gamma$) via two-photon fusion. Using wave functions from a four-body relativized quark model and NRQCD factorization, the authors compute radiative decay widths and cross sections for $^{208}\mathrm{Pb}+^{208}\mathrm{Pb}$ UPCs. They find that resonant contributions dominate over continuum in the $J/\psi J/\psi$ channel but are subdominant in the $\gamma\gamma$ channel—contradicting naive vector dominance expectations.

本文研究了全重四夸克态（特别是 $T_{4c}(0^{++}, 2^{++})$）在超外围重离子碰撞（$AA \to AA\, J/\psi J/\psi$ 和 $AA \to AA\, \gamma\gamma$）中通过双光子融合过程的产生。作者利用四体相对论夸克模型波函数和NRQCD因子化方法，计算了 $^{208}\mathrm{Pb}+^{208}\mathrm{Pb}$ 超外围碰撞中的辐射衰变宽度和截面。结果表明，在 $J/\psi J/\psi$ 通道中共振贡献主导连续谱，但在 $\gamma\gamma$ 通道中情况相反——这与朴素的矢量介子主导（vector dominance）图像明显矛盾。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from recent experimental observations (e.g., by CMS) of narrow structures like $X(6600)$ and $X(6900)$ in $J/\psi J/\psi$ invariant mass spectra, interpreted as candidates for fully-charmed tetraquarks. The paper aims to theoretically assess whether such states can be probed via two-photon processes in ultraperipheral collisions (UPCs), and to clarify the interplay between resonant and non-resonant (continuum) contributions in both $J/\psi J/\psi$ and $\gamma\gamma$ final states—particularly challenging simplistic models like vector meson dominance.
\n\n
动机源于CMS等实验最近在 $J/\psi J/\psi$ 不变量质量谱中观测到的窄结构（如 $X(6600)$ 和 $X(6900)$），这些被解释为全粲四夸克态的候选者。本文旨在从理论上评估这类态是否可通过超外围碰撞（UPC）中的双光子过程进行探测，并厘清在 $J/\psi J/\psi$ 和 $\gamma\gamma$ 末态中共振与非共振（连续谱）贡献之间的相互作用——尤其对矢量介子主导（vector meson dominance）等简化模型提出挑战。

Method: The authors employ an extended relativized quark model to obtain four-body wave functions for fully heavy tetraquarks. These wave functions yield long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) at the origin, which are then used within the NRQCD factorization framework to compute radiative decay widths $\Gamma(T_{4c} \to \gamma\gamma)$ and $\Gamma(T_{4c} \to J/\psi J/\psi)$. For the latter, simplified couplings are assumed due to model limitations. Cross sections for $^{208}\mathrm{Pb}+^{208}\mathrm{Pb}$ UPCs are calculated using the equivalent photon approximation, incorporating experimentally measured total widths ($\Gamma_{\rm{tot}} = 0.446\,\mathrm{GeV}$ for $X(6600)$, $0.135\,\mathrm{GeV}$ for $X(6900)$) in Breit-Wigner forms for resonant amplitudes. Continuum contributions are estimated separately and compared.

作者使用 2025 年 PDG 的 $M_Z$、$M_W$、$M_H$ 值以及 ATLAS-CMS 的顶夸克质量组合，计算比值 $ρ_{Zt} = M_Z M_t / M_H^2$。他们将极点层面的关系与在能标 $μ = M_t$ 处完整的 NNLO $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ 匹配进行比较，评估跑动耦合 $g_2$、$g_Y$、$y_t$ 和 $λ$。他们还检验了算术形式 $(M_W + M_t)/(2M_H)$，并推导出为使精确边界条件 $λ = g_Z y_t/(4\sqrt{2})$ 与实验数据一致所需的阈值修正因子 $κ_{\rm th}$。

This improvement is driven by the enhanced late-time acceleration in $w_0w_a$CDM, which suppresses the growth of structure and reduces the depth of gravitational potentials at intermediate redshifts ($z \sim 0.5–1$). The reduction is robust against changes in uncertainty modeling, confirming it is a genuine shift in the theoretical expectation within GR.

$w_0w_a$CDM 模型将 DES 第三年观测到的 Weyl potential 与基于广义相对论的预言之间的张力从 $Λ$CDM 下的约 $3σ$ 降低至 $1.6–2.2σ$，具体数值取决于使用 Planck 还是 ACT 的 CMB 引力透镜数据。这一改善源于 $w_0w_a$CDM 中更强的晚期加速膨胀，它抑制了结构增长，从而在中等红移（$z \sim 0.5–1$）处减小了引力势的深度。该缓解效应在不确定性建模变化下依然稳健，证实这是广义相对论框架内理论预言的真实偏移。

Conclusion: While evolving dark energy in the $w_0w_a$CDM framework alleviates the Weyl potential tension within General Relativity, it does not fully resolve it. The residual $1.6–2.2σ$ discrepancy suggests that either more precise data are needed to confirm the role of dynamical dark energy, or that additional physics—such as modifications to gravity or non-gravitational interactions in the dark sector—may be required. Notably, such extensions could also stabilize the phantom crossing behavior hinted at by DESI, offering a unified explanation for multiple observational anomalies.

尽管 $w_0w_a$CDM 框架中的演化暗能量可在广义相对论内缓解 Weyl potential 张力，但并未完全消除它。剩余的 $1.6–2.2σ$ 偏差表明，要么需要更高精度的数据以确认动力学暗能量的作用，要么可能需要引入额外物理机制——例如修改引力（modifications to gravity）或暗区非引力相互作用（non-gravitational interactions in the dark sector）。值得注意的是，此类扩展也可能稳定 DESI 暗示的幽灵穿越行为，从而为多个观测异常提供统一解释。

Abstract: Measurements from the Dark Energy Survey (DES) Year 3 data have shown that the Weyl potential -- the sum of the spatial and temporal distortions of the geometry -- evolves more slowly than predicted by General Relativity, assuming a $Λ$CDM background evolution. An evolving dark energy with a phantom crossing, as preferred by the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), is expected to decrease the depth of the gravitational potentials through a stronger acceleration than in $Λ$CDM, potentially solving the tension with General Relativity. In this paper, we show that $w_0w_a$CDM models indeed reduce the tension with respect to $Λ$CDM, down to a level of $1.6-2.2σ$, depending on the treatment of CMB lensing. This reduction is not due to an increase in the Weyl potential's uncertainties, but truly to the impact of the evolving background on the theoretical predictions in General Relativity. More data are needed to robustly determine if evolving dark energy fully explains the low value of the Weyl potential at intermediate redshifts, or if modifications of gravity or interactions in the dark sector are needed, which could simultaneously stabilize the phantom crossing indicated by DESI.

Method: The authors use a suite of validated Roman mock catalogs to measure the Void x CMB lensing cross-correlation under various configurations: 2D vs. 3D void definitions, rescaled vs. non-rescaled stacking profiles, different CMB map filtering schemes, and varying noise levels corresponding to Planck, Simons Observatory (SO), and CMB-S4-like experiments. They compute the signal-to-noise ratio (S/N) for each configuration and compare results across mock realizations to assess sensitivity to mock construction.

作者使用一组经过验证的Roman模拟星表，在多种配置下测量Void x CMB lensing交叉关联：2D与3D void定义、rescaled与non-rescaled stacking轮廓、不同的CMB图滤波方案，以及对应于Planck、Simons Observatory（SO）和CMB-S4类实验的不同噪声水平。他们计算每种配置下的信噪比（S/N），并通过比较不同模拟实现的结果来评估其对模拟构建的敏感性。

Result: The Void x CMB lensing signal shows minimal dependence on the choice of mock catalog, unlike galaxy or void auto-statistics, suggesting robustness against modeling uncertainties. The highest S/N is achieved with 2D voids and rescaled stacking. Void x CMB lensing信号对模拟星表的选择依赖性极小，与galaxy或void自相关统计不同，表明其对建模不确定性具有鲁棒性。2D voids结合rescaled stacking可获得最高信噪比。预测的信噪比为：与Planck联合时约为$\\sim13\\sigma$（8$\\sigma$），与SO联合时为$22\\sigma$（13$\\sigma$），与CMB-S4类巡天联合时达$31\\sigma$（18$\\sigma$）（括号内为3D voids结果）。这展示了强大的探测前景，但指出其对宇宙学参数的显式依赖仍有待建模。

Void x CMB lensing交叉关联是一种鲁棒的观测量，对模拟星表系统误差基本不敏感，结合Roman voids与下一代CMB巡天可实现高信噪比。尽管其宇宙学信息含量尚未完全量化，但本工作为未来结合LSS与CMB数据、首次从该探针获得直接宇宙学约束奠定了清晰路径。

Abstract: Gravitational lensing by large-scale structure imprints secondary anisotropies on the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) that can be exploited to probe cosmology. In particular, cosmic voids produce a characteristic lensing signature detectable through Void x CMB cross-correlations. This signal has been robustly measured in the past but its cosmological constraining power remains limited by the incomplete knowledge of how methodological choices affect its measurement and by its uncertain dependence on cosmological parameters. Using a set of validated Roman mock catalogs, we first quantify how mock construction impacts the measured signal and then forecast the capabilities of Roman, in combination with current and upcoming CMB surveys such as Planck, SO and CMB-S4-like experiments. We analyze the signal-to-noise ratio (S/N) for different void definitions (2D and 3D), stacking approaches (rescaled versus non-rescaled profiles), CMB map filtering schemes and noise levels. In contrast to galaxy and void statistics, we find that the Void x CMB lensing signal is less sensitive to the choice of mock catalog, indicating that future tensions with data are unlikely to stem from mock inaccuracies alone. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the limitations of Carr’s formula for the PBH mass function, which assumes uncorrelated Gaussian fluctuations and a sharp threshold for collapse. Method: The authors employ the excursion-set formalism, modeling the evolution of the smoothed density contrast $\\delta(R)$ as a stochastic trajectory driven by colored noise, where the correlation structure is determined by the chosen window function in Fourier space. They analyze several commonly used window functions (e.g., Gaussian, real- and k-space top-hat) and compute the first-crossing distribution of a critical threshold $\\delta_c$, which yields the PBH formation probability and hence the mass function via $M \\propto R^3$. The analysis accounts for non-Markovianity induced by noise correlations.
\n\n
作者采用excursion-set形式体系，将平滑后的密度对比度$\\delta(R)$的演化建模为由colored noise驱动的随机轨迹，其关联结构由Fourier空间中所选的window function决定。他们分析了几种常用的window function（如Gaussian、实空间和k空间top-hat），并计算了穿越临界阈值$\\delta_c$的首穿分布（first-crossing distribution），由此得到PBH形成概率，并通过$M \\propto R^3$导出质量函数。该分析考虑了由noise关联引起的非Markov性。

Result: The main result is that the low-mass tail ($M \\ll M_*$) of the PBH mass function deviates significantly from Carr’s prediction due to noise correlations, which eliminate the degeneracy between different trajectories leading to collapse. Specifically, the mass function exhibits a steeper or shallower fall-off depending on the window function’s smoothness in Fourier space. Near the characteristic mass scale $M_*$, however, Carr’s formula remains accurate if a smooth window (e.g., Gaussian) is used, as correlations become subdominant.

主要结果是：由于noise关联破坏了导致坍缩的不同轨迹之间的简并性，PBH质量函数在低质量端（$M \\ll M_*$）显著偏离Carr公式的预测。具体而言，质量函数的衰减行为（更陡或更缓）取决于window function在Fourier空间中的光滑程度。然而，在特征质量尺度$M_*$附近，若采用光滑的window function（如Gaussian），Carr公式仍然准确，因为此时关联效应变得次要。

Abstract: We study the mass function of primordial black holes (PBHs) within the excursion-set theory, in which the response of the stochastic density contrast to the variation of the coarse-graining scale is described by colored noises. For several window functions often used in the literature, we investigate how this choice affects the formation probability as well as the resultant mass function of PBHs. It is found that the low-mass tail of the mass function differs from the one predicted from Carr's formula. The difference comes from the prevalence of correlated noises, by which degeneracy of the formation probabilities ceases to exist. Nevertheless, Carr's formula still provides a practical estimation in the vicinity of the characteristic mass scale, as long as a smooth window function in Fourier space is used.

Abstract: The recent James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations reported the unexpectedly large abundance of massive galaxies with stellar masses of $\sim 10^{10}~M_{\odot}$ at high redshifts $z \simeq 6.5 - 9$ compared with the prediction of the standard $Λ$CDM model. 将暗光子与标准光子耦合提供了一种理论上稳健且极简的原初磁生成机制，克服了现有方法的关键局限。该机制不仅能解释观测到的星系际磁场，还通过隐藏扇区物理为解决更广泛的宇宙学开放问题开辟了通道。

Abstract: Magnetic fields observed across cosmic scales are difficult to explain within conventional physics. A primordial origin is, thus, often assumed. While a nonminimal coupling of the inflaton with the electromagnetic field could theoretically generate magnetic fields of about $10^{-13}$ G, this approach faces significant issues, including strong-coupling and backreaction problems. ``Dark photons", arising naturally in hidden-sector extensions of the Standard Model, provide a well-motivated framework for addressing various cosmic as well as particle physics issues. We demonstrate that coupling dark photons with standard ones can result in adequate magnetogenesis without the limitations of existing models. This minimal mechanism may also provide insights into unresolved cosmic mysteries.

TL;DR: Cosmological observations at ~2.5–3σ hint that dark energy may be dynamical. Using a CPL parametrization for the background and allowing scale- and redshift-dependent modifications to gravity below a comoving cutoff $λ_c$, the authors find that structure growth suppression consistent with CMB and late-time probes requires $λ_c \sim \mathcal{O}(10)\,\mathrm{Mpc}$. Including such modified gravity strengthens the statistical preference for a dynamical CPL background over $Λ$CDM, pushing its equation-of-state parameters slightly deeper into the quintom regime.
\n\n
宇宙学观测在约 2.5–3σ 置信水平上暗示暗能量可能是动力学的。作者采用 CPL 参数化描述背景演化，并允许引力耦合在共动截断尺度 $λ_c$ 以下随尺度和红移发生偏离。他们发现，为了在符合 CMB（尤其是低 $\ell$ 的 late-time ISW 效应和高 $\ell$ 的透镜效应）约束的同时抑制低红移结构增长，修改引力效应必须出现在 $λ_c \sim \mathcal{O}(10)\,\mathrm{Mpc}$ 以下的尺度上。结合 Planck PR4、DESI DR2、Pantheon+（或 DES-Dovekie）及红移空间畸变数据，包含此类修改引力效应后，动力学 CPL 背景对 $Λ$CDM 的统计偏好从中等增强至轻度强烈，并使其状态方程参数略微更深地进入 quintom 区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current cosmological datasets (CMB, BAO, SNe Ia) show a ~2.5–3σ indication that dark energy is not a cosmological constant but dynamical. However, both $Λ$CDM and the standard CPL model exhibit mild tension with redshift-space distortion (RSD) measurements of structure growth. This motivates exploring whether late-time modifications to General Relativity—specifically, a scale- and redshift-dependent effective gravitational coupling—can reconcile the background expansion history with observed structure formation while respecting CMB constraints.
\n\n
当前宇宙学数据集（CMB、BAO、Ia 型超新星）显示出约 2.5–3σ 的迹象，表明暗能量并非宇宙学常数，而是动力学的。然而，$Λ$CDM 和标准 CPL 模型均与红移空间畸变（RSD）对结构增长的测量存在轻微张力。这促使研究者探索晚期对广义相对论的修改——特别是有效引力耦合随尺度和红移的变化——是否能在满足 CMB 约束的同时，协调背景膨胀历史与观测到的结构形成。

Method: The authors adopt a CPL parametrization $w(z) = w_0 + w_a z/(1+z)$ for the dark energy equation of state as the background. They introduce a phenomenological modification to gravity by allowing the effective gravitational coupling $G_{\rm eff}(k,z)$ to deviate from Newton’s constant $G_N$ only for comoving wavenumbers $k > 2\pi/λ_c$, i.e., on scales smaller than $λ_c$. This deviation is modeled with two redshift bins: $0 \leq z < 1$ and $1 \leq z \leq 3$, each with its own amplitude parameter. The model is constrained using Planck PR4 CMB data (including low-$\ell$ ISW and high-$\ell$ lensing), DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+ (or DES-Dovekie) supernovae, and RSD measurements. The analysis jointly fits $w_0$, $w_a$, $λ_c$, and the two modification amplitudes.
\n\n
作者采用 CPL 参数化 $w(z) = w_0 + w_a z/(1+z)$ 描述暗能量状态方程作为背景。他们引入一种唯象的引力修改：仅当共动波数 $k > 2\pi/λ_c$（即尺度小于 $λ_c$）时，有效引力耦合 $G_{\rm eff}(k,z)$ 才偏离牛顿常数 $G_N$。该偏离通过两个红移区间建模：$0 \leq z < 1$ 和 $1 \leq z \leq 3$，每个区间有独立的幅度参数。模型使用 Planck PR4 CMB 数据（包括低 $\ell$ 的 late-time ISW 效应和高 $\ell$ 的透镜效应）、DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+（或 DES-Dovekie）超新星以及 RSD 测量进行约束。分析联合拟合 $w_0$、$w_a$、$λ_c$ 和两个修改幅度参数。

Result: The analysis finds that to suppress late-time structure growth sufficiently to match RSD data while remaining consistent with CMB constraints, modified gravity effects must be confined to scales smaller than $λ_c \sim \mathcal{O}(10)\,\mathrm{Mpc}$. A CPL background with standard GR is moderately preferred over $Λ$CDM ($\Delta \chi^2 \sim -4$ to $-6$). When the modified gravity parameters are included, this preference strengthens to a mildly strong level ($\Delta \chi^2 \sim -8$ to $-10$). The best-fit CPL parameters shift slightly toward the quintom region ($w < -1$ at early times and $w > -1$ at late times), though remain consistent with previous estimates.
\n\n
分析发现，为在符合 CMB 约束的同时充分抑制晚期结构增长以匹配 RSD 数据，修改引力效应必须限制在 $λ_c \sim \mathcal{O}(10)\,\mathrm{Mpc}$ 以下的尺度。具有标准 GR 的 CPL 背景对 $Λ$CDM 表现出中等偏好（$\Delta \chi^2 \sim -4$ 至 $-6$）。当引入修改引力参数后，该偏好增强至轻度强烈水平（$\Delta \chi^2 \sim -8$ 至 $-10$）。最佳拟合的 CPL 参数略微向 quintom 区域移动（早期 $w < -1$，晚期 $w > -1$），但仍与先前估计一致。

Conclusion: Dynamical dark energy in the form of a CPL parametrization is statistically favored over $Λ$CDM at the ~2.5–3σ level when combined with late-time cosmological probes. Allowing for scale- and redshift-dependent modifications to gravity on sub-10 Mpc scales further improves the fit, primarily by alleviating the tension in structure growth measurements without violating CMB constraints. This suggests that a combination of dynamical dark energy and small-scale modified gravity may provide a more complete description of cosmic acceleration.
\n\n
当结合晚期宇宙学探针时，以 CPL 参数化形式出现的动力学暗能量在 ~2.5–3σ 水平上统计优于 $Λ$CDM。允许在小于 10 Mpc 尺度上引入随尺度和红移变化的引力修改可进一步改善拟合，主要通过缓解结构增长测量中的张力，同时不违反 CMB 约束。这表明，动力学暗能量与小尺度修改引力的结合可能为宇宙加速提供更完整的描述。

Abstract: Cosmological data from the cosmic microwave background (CMB), baryon acoustic oscillations, and Type Ia supernovae suggest that the component driving the accelerated expansion of the Universe may be dynamical at the $\sim 2.5$-$3σ$ CL. The best-fit CPL model produces a level of cosmic structure similar to that of $Λ$CDM, with both models exhibiting mild tension with redshift-space distortion data. In this {\it Letter}, we parametrize possible departures of the effective gravitational coupling from Newton's constant in the late Universe, below a comoving scale $λ_c$, using two redshift bins, $0 \leq z < 1$ and $1 \leq z \leq 3$. We then determine the optimal values of $λ_c$ and the amplitude of these deviations from General Relativity, assuming a background with dynamical dark energy in CPL form. We find that, in order to achieve the required suppression of structure growth at low redshifts while remaining consistent with CMB constraints -- primarily from the late-time ISW effect at low $\ell$ and lensing at high $\ell$ -- modified gravity effects must appear on scales smaller than $λ_c \sim \mathcal{O}(10)\,\mathrm{Mpc}$. Using Planck PR4, DESI DR2, Pantheon+ (or DES-Dovekie) and redshift-space distortions data we confirm that a CPL background with standard gravity is moderately preferred over $Λ$CDM; this preference strengthens to a mildly strong level when modified gravity effects are included. This enhancement leaves the CPL parameters largely unchanged, but shifts them slightly further into the quintom region.

</details>
